      - **Display rule (one truth table, `TechErrorNumberDisplayTest.ABBREV_TABLE` read by `techErrorNumberDisplay.test.js`):** `TechErrorFormat.abbreviated(value, exp, en)` = JS `formatAbbreviated(n, exp, lang)`: the value shifted by 10^exp on the DECIMAL string (`BigDecimal.movePointLeft`; JS `shiftedDigits` on `decimalParts` — no float division), trailing zeros dropped, Qlik letter k / M / G / T in both languages, decimal separator of the language, no space ("4,4G" / "4.4G"; "4.40G" comes back "4,4G"). The part before the letter is NEVER grouped, anywhere (review 2026-10-18: "4.400M" in the PDF / mail / tiles / summary / history / read-only cells / previous-month hint re-parsed in the editor as 4,4 million — a single separator before a suffix is the decimal — so copying it entered a value 1000× too small): "4400M", "1250G", "1000T"; an ungrouped string parses back to the stored value in BOTH languages (round-trip test over `ABBREV_TABLE` + generated values). A value that is not `abbreviable` (shift needs > 6 decimals) prints the full number (`number()`), and the client never sends its unit (`ReportSections.storedExp`; `canonicalValues` filters it like the server, the input keeps the typed "4,123456789G" while typing and shows the full number after a reload). `compactQlik` and the trend label `compact` / `compactNumber` are ungrouped before the letter too ("2000T", "1250T"). Applied wherever the value is drawn as a figure: PDF + mail metric values, summary `{f:}` (`TechErrorSummary.line(…, units, …)` / JS `summaryLine(…, units)`, also `facts`, `auto`, `blocks`), tiles (`TechErrorTiles.build(…, units)` / `summaryTiles(…, units)`), issue counts (`TechErrorFormat.count(n, lang, unit)` — also the anomaly lead sentence and card chips, `TechErrorIssueText.lead(…, errorsUnit, customersUnit)` / JS `issueLeadText` + `formatCount`), table number cells (`cell(c, v, lang, unit)`), mail value tiles, mail preview / test mail (same code), history VALUES previews. `{d:}` and the tile delta of a field whose CURRENT value has a unit: `TechErrorFormat.compactQlik` / JS `compactQlik` (unit by magnitude, ≤ 2 decimals, rounding to 1000 moves up — "+50M (+%1,15)"; `QLIK_TABLE`); the percent part and point deltas are unchanged; no unit → byte-identical output. History: a unit change is a change (`TechErrorChangeDiff`: "4,4G" → "4.400.000.000" row; fingerprint of a unit value = `"<value>@<exp>"`, unit-less fingerprints unchanged, so the auto-save merge sees 4,4G → full → 4,4G as reverted). Trend bar labels / analytics Y axis (`compact` / `compactNumber`) switched to k / M / G / T in both languages (`TechErrorTrendTest.compactLabels`).
      - **Editor:** `NumberInput` takes `unit` (undefined = not unit-aware, e.g. the template target) and emits `onChange(n, exp)` (`storedExp` = `abbreviationExp` when `abbreviable`, else null); a unit-aware input re-syncs its text when the same number arrives with another (shown) exponent (discard, server dropped it). `ReportEditor` keeps `draft.units` (`setField(id, v, exp)`, `setCell(…, exp)`, `setUnit` for issue counts via `IssuesBlock onUnit`); `canonicalValues` filters units exactly like the server (`unitPlaceValue` + `abbreviable`) — no false "unsaved changes"; `revertInvalidValues` reverts the unit with the value. Read-only viewers see the abbreviated text (disabled input, table `te-cell-readonly`). The previous-month hint uses the PREVIOUS report's unit (`previous.units`, controller `previousDto` + `TechErrorReportService.sameTypeUnits`, only the same-type field keys).
      - **Zero tick (`TechErrorFormat.zeroOk(template, field, v)` = JS `zeroOk(field, v, templateFields)`; field rule `ZERO_TABLE`, template rule `RATE_BASE_FIELDS` / `RATE_BASE_TABLE`, exact-0 core `zeroCount` / `COUNT_TABLE`):** a NUMBER field (no calc) whose value is exactly 0 (a Number — empty / unknown is not 0), whose `effectiveBetter` is not `higher`, and that is not ONLY a rate base (`rateBaseOnly`, review 2026-10-18 user decision: the `den` of some `ratio` calc and never the `num` / `minus` of any `ratio` — the `of` of `change` / `increase` is ignored, the base's own change is tracked: default `total-tx` → no tick ("0 transactions" is no data, not "no issues"), `error-count` (numerator of the error rate, base of the unmapped / quota rates), `unmapped-count`, `quota-count`, `outage-errors` → tick; no template migration, tile colours unchanged). Every drawing path passes the template (PDF / mail `in.template()`, `TechErrorTiles.build`, `summaryTiles`, `MetricField` `templateFields`). Issue (anomaly topic) counts get NO tick anywhere (review 2026-10-18 decision — PDF / mail never drew it). Never on percent / yes-no / text, never inside summary sentences, never for exempt values (not drawn). In the editor the tick shows only while the field is not invalid and its CURRENT text is complete and parses to exactly 0 (`NumberInput`: `showZero` — "0x" / "-" hide it; the stored value alone is not enough). Shown: editor (lucide `CircleCheck`, `text-success`, inside the input on the right (`pr-9`), `aria-hidden` icon + sr-only "Sıfır — sorun yok" / "Zero — no issues" (`te.zero.ok`) in the input's `aria-describedby`, `data-slot="te-zero-ok"`), summary tiles on screen / PDF / mail (`Tile.zero`), PDF metric values (`PdfCanvas.zeroTick`: green-700 #15803d filled circle + white tick, vector, `ZERO_D` 10 pt / tiles 9.5 pt, 4 pt after the clipped value — the value gets the room, never overlaps; graphics state saved), mail value tiles + tiles (`mail/TechErrorZeroMark`: a 32 × 32 PNG generated ONCE (AWT, failure → text ✓ fallback), CID `te-zero-ok`, `width="16" height="16"` + alt, ONE inline attachment per mail however many zeros (`buildFull` adds it only when the body references it); plain text " ✓ (sıfır — sorun yok)"). Not drawn in the PDF issue-count line or table cells. `themeContrast.test.js` checks `--success` against `--background` / `--card` ≥ 3:1 in every theme.
      - **Rollback / rolling deploy:** a pre-2026-10-18 pod ignores `units` (lenient read) and DROPS it from a report saved through it → the report shows full numbers again (values are unchanged); it also draws no tick and uses the old trend letters. Nothing else is lost. An AUTOSAVE that lands on such a pod is treated as saved (`accepted {sent, base}` matches the sent content, units included) — deliberately: treating the dropped units as unsaved would re-send the same content every 30 s against the old pod (endless autosave loop), so the editor keeps the abbreviated text until the report is reopened, then shows the full numbers; numbers typed before this release keep showing in full (they have no unit).
      - **Gates:** `TechErrorNumberDisplayTest` (ABBREV / QLIK / ZERO / RATE_BASE / COUNT / UNIT_SUMMARY / UNIT_TILES tables, `rateBaseOnlyIsNotTicked` incl. the TR + EN default template, `abbreviableRule`), `TechErrorValuesTest.unitsAreValidatedSilently|unitsDroppedWhenAbbreviationWouldRound`, `TechErrorChangeDiffTest.unitChangeIsAChange`, `TechErrorReportServiceTest.unitsSavedChangedCarriedAndPrevious`, `TechErrorReportControllerTest.readReportCarriesUnits`, `TechErrorReportPdfWriterTest.abbreviatedNumbersAndZeroTicks` (text + vector tick count + no glyph overlap + mail CID once + no tick on "0 transactions" in PDF and mail), `TechErrorReportPdfWriterTest.technicalErrorCountTypedAbbreviatedReachesExecutiveSummary` (the user's words: "4,4G" typed into the technical error count — DEFAULT TR + EN template, save filter → stored JSON → live calc — shows in the PDF executive-summary sentence, its tile and the "Verileri" value, and in the mail summary text, tiles and value tiles), `TechErrorTrendTest.compactLabels` ("1250T"), `EmailSamples` (`report-tech-error-tr`: "12,5M" + a zero), `techErrorNumberDisplay.test.js` (round trip: every abbreviated output parses back in BOTH languages), `TechErrorNumberDisplay.test.jsx` (rate base, "0x" / "-", no issue tick, "4,123456789G" saved without a unit, "0G" / "0k" saved without a unit and "0" on screen), `techErrorTrendModel.test.js`, `themeContrast.test.js` (graphic pairs), `e2e/channel-reports.spec.js` ("kısaltılmış sayı ve sıfır işareti", 390/768/1280: type "4.4G", save, reload → "4,4G" + full number underneath, "4,4G" typed into the technical error count shows at once in the summary sentence preview and its tile and is saved with `error-count: 9`, previous-month hint "4350M" (never "4.350M"), tick inside the input, no tick on the anomaly count / "0x" / total-tx 0, tile fits, no clipping / overflow, touch targets).
    - **Collapsible sections + report autosave (2026-10-16, user decisions):** report AND template editor sections are collapsible (`SectionCollapse.jsx`: shadcn Collapsible, `CollapsibleCard`, `SectionOpenContext`, `useSectionOpenState`, `ExpandAllButton`; the toggle is a Button INSIDE the section `h3` — WAI pattern, the copy icon stays a sibling). Closed by default; opened ids per report / channel in personal storage `sm.te.open.r.<report>` / `sm.te.open.t.<channel>` (`{all:true}` or `{ids}`, ≤ 64; prefix `sm.te.open.` in `PERSONAL_PREFIXES`; anomaly topics have their own `sm.te.open.ri.<report>` since 2026-10-18 — see "Topic accordion"). Content stays MOUNTED when closed (typed invalid text, focus and the table layout survive — `TableBlock` keeps its last layout at width 0); hidden with `hidden="until-found"` where supported (jsdom too — closed content is NOT accessible in tests; seed `openAllSections()` from `helpers/techErrorFixtures.js` after `localStorage.clear()`), else `data-[state=closed]:hidden`; `beforematch` opens the section. A section whose error set NEWLY gains a key opens (`reportErrorSection` / `templateErrorSection` + `errorCountsBySection`; the counts Map changes only when the error set does — `ImagesBlock` must not re-render per keystroke); a closed section shows `te.sec.invalid`; Save / PDF / send refused for invalid fields open those sections. Report autosave (`ReportEditor`, `AUTOSAVE_MS` 30 s + `visibilitychange` hidden): only when dirty, editable, no conflict, no other action / save / load running; silent (status line `te-save-status`, no toast); invalid client fields are sent with their STORED value (`revertInvalidValues`); a 4xx / validation refusal is not retried with the same content (`autoBlocked`), network / 5xx retries next tick, 409 shows the conflict banner and stops it. Every save goes through ONE lock (`savePromise`: manual save, `ensureSaved`, `leave` wait for a running save — never two PUTs with the same version). `PUT /reports/{id}` takes `auto: true`: `TechErrorHistoryService.recordValues(…, auto)` MERGES into the latest event when it is the same actor's auto `VALUES` event and its `first_at` is within `AUTO_MERGE_WINDOW` 15 min (a fixed window from the FIRST save — a long session writes a row + an audit entry every 15 min). Rows merge by a STABLE KEY (`TechErrorChangeDiff.Change.key`: `f:<field>`, `i:<sid>:<issue>:<item|topic|title|desc|customers|errors>`, `i:<sid>:order`, `t:<sid>:<row>:<col>`, `x:<sid>`, `s:<sid>` — never the display label: two "Başlıksız konu" rows collided) and a RAW-value fingerprint (`bh`/`ah`, "0" empty, "1" issue present, else SHA-256 prefix — previews are lossy: bold / line breaks / 4 decimals): first `before` + newest `after` per key, op recomputed, a row whose fingerprints match is dropped, part rows of an issue added or removed in the span fold into its add / remove row, key-less legacy rows are never merged; nothing left → the event STAYS with `reverted: true` (a sent report's "changed" state stays explained); an `unavailable` diff merges too. The controller audits `TECH_ERROR_REPORT_UPDATE` only when no merge happened (`SaveOutcome.mergedHistory`). Client (review round 1): an autosave response NEVER replaces the draft (it trimmed a trailing space / "- " mid-typing); `accepted {sent, base}` + `isSavedJson` treat the server-normalised content as saved and stop re-sending it; network / 5xx / timeout back off 30 s → ≤ 5 min; the autosave PUT has `AUTOSAVE_TIMEOUT_MS` 30 s (`saveReport(…, {auto, timeoutMs})` — a hung request froze Back / Save / PDF); `load` awaits a running save; half-typed numbers report through `PendingInputContext` (`NumberInput pendingKey`, cleared on unmount) and count as invalid (sent with the stored value) and unsaved; the status line says when autosave is paused (conflict) or blocked (refused content); Discard is hidden while an autosave runs; new issues get unique ids (`newIssue`: `i-<time><rand>` — a reused `i-<n>` merged a replaced topic away). Rolling deploy: an old pod ignores `auto` (one history row + audit per 30 s until the rollout ends). Review round 2: restoring a backup whose version ≠ base PAUSES autosave (`autoHold`, status `te.autoHeldRestore`) until a manual Save / Discard / full load — it used to overwrite a colleague's newer save within 30 s; an autosave never removes the found-draft banner (its last copy); applyTemplate uses `isSavedJson`; a non-field 4xx shows the server text (`te.autoBlockedReason`); `leave` ignores repeated clicks while it waits; issue rows carry `subject` (the topic's current name) so a merged "added" row shows the latest name; a merged event with one unavailable diff still lists its rows. Known limit: an autosave that times out after the server committed makes the next save 409 (conflict banner; nothing is lost — backup + "load latest"). History shows `te.hist.auto` + `te.hist.autoSaves`. The template is NOT autosaved. Gates: `TechErrorReportServiceTest.autoSavesMergeInHistory|mergeChangesRules`, `TechErrorReportControllerTest.saveReportAuditsOncePerAutoWindow`, `techErrorSections.test.js`, `SectionCollapse.test.jsx`, `TechErrorReportAutosave.test.jsx`, `TechErrorTemplateCollapse.test.jsx`, `TechErrorHistory.test.jsx` (auto marker), `e2e/channel-reports.spec.js`.
    - **Summary-card figures + anomaly naming (2026-10-17, release 4a, user decisions: "map yapılmamış ve kota hatalarının girişi yönetici özeti bölümünde olsun, ayrı bölüm olmasın"; "anomali konularının isimlendirmesi daha iyi olsun"):** a `metrics` section may carry `host` = the id of a `summary` section of the same template (strict save: must exist and be a summary → 400; lenient read drops a bad one; an OLDER pod ignores the key → the section just shows as a normal section, nothing is lost — that is why the fields stay in metrics sections instead of moving into the summary). An embedded (hosted) section is drawn INSIDE the summary card in the report editor (its fields with every metrics behaviour, after the generated text), never as its own card / PDF section; `allFields`, values, calc, history diff, trend, carry and previous-month hints are unchanged (still metrics). Summary output is BLOCKS (`TechErrorSummary.blocks` = JS `summaryBlocks`, one truth table `TechErrorSummaryBlocksTest.BLOCKS_TABLE` read by `techErrorSummaryBlocks.test.js`): text + note blocks — an embedded section's text / textarea values (not blank, not exempt) are placed right AFTER the first kept sentence that references any of its fields (the next sentence starts a new paragraph), unplaced ones at the end; a manual override = override text + all notes at the end ("edit by hand" copies only `auto()` text — notes never freeze in the override, and they are outside the 12 000 summary cap). PDF / mail print a note under its FIELD LABEL (small bold, ≤ 2 lines in the PDF; "Açıklama" / "Note" only when the label is blank — review 2026-10-17: a constant heading made two notes indistinguishable) + rich text; a short `text` field is never a note (it is an untold-value line when no sentence tells it); mail tiles start at the first NON-embedded metrics section. Error → section mapping sends an embedded field's error to the host card; expand-all ignores embedded sections. Template editor: "Gösterim yeri" per metrics section, badge, removing a hosting summary asks and clears hosts (`sanitizeHosts`), version diff row `section.host`. Default template: `unmapped` / `quota` sections hosted by `exec-summary` right after it (count + "… hakkında açıklama"), counts removed from `summary` (Verileri); issues renamed ("Anormal Artış Gösteren Hatalar", "{konu}: Hata Artışı", "Ne oldu? (zaman, yer, etki)", "Etkilenen müşteri sayısı", "Bu konudan kaynaklı teknik hata adedi"); the lenient fallbacks for MISSING labels stay old (stored data keeps its meaning). One-time `TechErrorSummaryEmbedMigration` (KEY `2026-10-17-tech-error-summary-embed` — never rename after release; waits for the calc migration's marker; exact old defaults only, TR + EN, both production shapes: counts in Verileri + note-only sections, or count + note sections; per attribute for the issue labels; idempotent). Rollback note: a pre-4a pod shows hosted sections as normal sections; the migrated layout stays. **Untold values (review 2026-10-17):** a number / percent / yes-no field of an embedded section whose value is set but which NO kept sentence references is printed as its own line "Label: **value**" (number / percent bold; yes/no and short text plain, "**" runs in text neutralised; a label ending in "?" only drops the colon) right where the section's notes go — first pass collects kept sentences + referenced ids (the text cap stops pass 1 too, so a capped sentence never marks a field as told), second pass places blocks (`TechErrorSummary.facts` = JS `summaryFacts`); a section whose placement adds NOTHING never breaks the paragraph (the all-counts-no-notes default report stays ONE paragraph, byte-identical to `auto()`); a manual override gets notes only. Migrations skip a channel deleted between read and save (`NoSuchElementException`); a client save with a blank anomaly label gets the 2026-10-17 default names (lenient stored reads keep the old names). Gates: `TechErrorSummaryBlocksTest` (BLOCKS_TABLE, read by `techErrorSummaryBlocks.test.js`), `TechErrorSummaryEmbedMigrationTest` (both shapes → today's default byte-identical, changed sections kept, H2 job, waits for the calc marker), `TechErrorTemplateTest.hostStrictAndLenient`, `TechErrorReportPdfWriterTest.summaryEmbeddedNotesAndFacts`, `techErrorSummaryEmbed.test.js`, `TechErrorSummaryEmbed.test.jsx`, `e2e/channel-reports.spec.js` ("özet kartına gömülü", 390/768/1280).
    - **SLA target + colour tiles + comparison sentences (2026-10-17, release 4b, user decisions: tiles default "6 karışık", colour "hedef + yön", SLA "alanda hedef + otomatik hesap", sentences "yer tutucu {d:alan}"):** a percent field may carry `target` (0–100) + `better` (lower|higher|none; `better` alone is also allowed on number fields) — strict save: target only on percent and it needs `better`; lenient read drops a bad one. `TechErrorTemplate.effectiveTarget(field, targets)` = the report's `values.targets[id]` else the template target (pure: an override is honoured as given; `TechErrorValues` stores an override ONLY for percent fields with a template target, 0–100 → 400 `targets.<id>`, otherwise dropped silently; the key is written only when non-empty; `carry` keeps same-id percent targets; history row `g:<field>` "<label> — bu ayın hedefi"); `effectiveBetter` defaults percent → lower, number → none. New calc kind `compare` (yesno; `of` = a percent field WITH a target, computed percents allowed — `TechErrorCalc.apply` runs two passes; equality = met; no target / not percent → BAD_REF, no value → MISSING): the default `sla-above` is computed from `sla-rate` (target 99.95, higher), so it can no longer be a `skip_if_no` target (tests link to `had-outage`). Summary refs: `{d:id}` previous-month delta (`TechErrorFormat.delta`: counts "+1.250 (+%3,2)" bold, "değişim yok" plain, prev 0 → count only; percents in points `TechErrorFormat.points` "−0,05 puan" / "pts"), `{t:id}` effective target (needs no value), `{dt:id}` value − target in points; a sentence drops when the previous month, the target or the value is missing; `TechErrorSummary.refs/malformed` know all four prefixes; the previous month comes from `TechErrorPdfInput.previous` (`pdfInput` computes `previousFields` once; `summaryBlocks(..., previous, targets)`). Tiles: `summary.tiles` (≤ `MAX_TILES` 6 field ids, dedup, existence NOT checked — a removed field is skipped at draw time) → `TechErrorTiles.build` (value, delta + dir, tone: target → good/bad by met, else the better direction vs previous (none / no delta / flat → neutral), null value → "—" unknown; exempt / non-numeric skipped) drawn at the top of the summary in the PDF (rows of 3, `PdfCanvas.triangle` + delta + "hedef %x"), in the mail (`tilesHtml` `data-slot="te-tiles"` `data-tone`; the 16-stat chain itself was removed in release 5) and on screen (`SummaryTiles`, `data-slot="te-tiles"` / `te-tile`, tone name for screen readers). Default TR/EN template: `sla-rate` target 99.95 higher, `sla-above` compare, exec-summary tiles [error-rate, total-tx, error-count, unmapped-count, quota-count, sla-rate], the sentence "Önceki aya göre teknik hata adedi {d:error-count}, teknik hata oranı {d:error-rate} değişmiştir." and the SLA sentence with `{t:}` / `{dt:}`. One-time `TechErrorSlaTilesMigration` (KEY `2026-10-17-tech-error-sla-target-tiles` — never rename after release; waits for the summary-embed marker; adds target / better / compare / tiles / new default lines only where the old default is still in place). UI: report `MetricField` shows "Bu ayın hedefi (%)" (`data-field="targets.<id>"`, pending / revert keys `targets.`) under a percent field with a template target; compare `CalcOutput` note `te.calc.formulaCompare`; the pending list says `te.sum.needsPrevious`; template `TargetEditor` (Hedef + Daha iyi; errors show after a save attempt like the other template errors), `CalcEditor` compare kind with a target-percent picker, `TilesEditor` (pick + add, move, remove, "n / 6"), the "Karşılaştırma ekle" menu inserts `{d:}` / `{t:}` / `{dt:}` (the "Alan ekle" menu is unchanged), previews label refs by kind (`te.tpl.refKind.*`); version diff rows `field.target|better`, `summary.tiles`. JS mirrors: `effectiveTarget` / `effectiveBetter` / `deltaOf` / `pointsOf` / `summaryTiles`, `summaryLineStatus(..., previous, targets)` (`needsPrevious`, reasons `notNumeric` / `noTarget`), `calcResults(..., targets)` two-pass, `formatNumber(n, lang, maxFraction)`. Truth tables read from the Java source through `test/helpers/javaTable.js`: `TechErrorTilesTest.TILES_TABLE` (`techErrorSlaTiles.test.js`), `TechErrorSummaryDeltaTest.DELTA_TABLE` (`techErrorSummaryDelta.test.js`), `TechErrorCalcTest.TABLE` rows `id=type@target@better`. Gates: `TechErrorTilesTest`, `TechErrorSummaryDeltaTest`, `TechErrorSlaTilesMigrationTest`, `TechErrorCalcTest.compareUsesReportTargetAndComputedPercent`, `TechErrorTemplateTest.targetBetterAndTilesRules`, `TechErrorValuesTest.targets`, `TechErrorChangeDiffTest.targets`, `TechErrorReportPdfWriterTest.tilesInPdfAndMail`, `TechErrorReportServiceTest.targetsSavedCarriedAndPreviousForTiles`, `TechErrorSlaTiles.test.jsx`, `e2e/channel-reports.spec.js` ("SLA hedefi ve renkli kutucuklar", 390/768/1280). **Rollback note:** a pre-4b pod knows none of `compare`, `target` / `better`, `tiles`, `values.targets` or the `{d:}` / `{t:}` / `{dt:}` refs — it drops the compare calc (the lenient read keeps the field, so `sla-above` becomes a manual question), ignores target / better / tiles, DROPS `targets` from a report saved through it, and prints `{d:…}` tokens literally in the summary text (screen, PDF, mail). Avoid editing channel templates / reports during the rollout; never roll back past 4b without telling the teams (the migration has already rewritten the default lines). The migration runs 4 minutes after the FIRST new pod boots, i.e. usually while old pods still serve: a report CREATED on an old pod in that window copies the new lines but loses target / compare / tiles (lenient read) — "apply latest template" repairs it. Review rules (2026-10-17): a target with `better: none` is rejected (400; lenient read drops the target) and `compare` / tiles treat `none` as not comparable (BAD_REF / neutral) — the template editor hides "Yön yok" while a target is set and tracks an unparseable target text as a client error so "Şablonu kaydet" is refused (`TplClientErrorContext`); the report's month target is checked 0–100 next to the field; the summary "told" set counts only `{f:}` refs (a field named only by `{d:}` keeps its untold-value line); the PDF keeps the summary title with the first tile row and never draws a tile above the top margin (`PdfCanvas.pageTop`); the mail tile class follows the per-row count (`tile3` / `tile` / none); the migration gives the `{d:}` sentence also to channels without a usable `sla-rate` (old SLA line kept); removing a field or a section drops its fields from `summary.tiles` (`pruneRemovedTiles`, both confirms say so) and a dangling tile id shows "silinmiş alan". Round 3: `effectiveTarget` (Java AND JS) honours a report override ONLY when the template field has a target — the storage rule, so a stale backup target never revives a sentence / tile the PDF would not show; typing a target on a field whose direction is "none" drops the direction (`withTarget`), so the select really shows "Yön seçin".
    - **E-mail redesign (2026-10-17, release 5, user decision S5: "Outlook'ta çok dar; PDF içeriğinin benzeri e-postanın İÇİNDE; görseller e-postada da (≤ 1000 px, tam çözünürlük PDF ekinde); genişlik 1000 px, telefonda tek sütun; koyu lacivert başlık bandı + iyi yeşil / dikkat turuncu / kötü kırmızı; yönetici sunumu gibi sade"):** `TechErrorReportMail` builds the body from the SAME `TechErrorPdfInput` as the PDF, in TEMPLATE ORDER: `MailDoc.extraWide()` card (`MailTokens.WIDTH_XWIDE` 1000; `CONTENT_W` 950), navy band `data-slot="te-mail-band"` (uppercase kicker · period, title, team · channel · period, "Hazırlayan · Üretim (Türkiye saati)"), one line naming the attached PDF, then `summary` (tiles strip `te-tiles` — every tile `class="stack"` so phones get ONE column — + blocks + embedded-section notes), `metrics` (hosted sections skipped; value tiles `te-values`, 3 per row, exempt fields hidden, an all-exempt section prints the "muaf" note; textarea fields as label + rich text), `issues` (cards `te-issue`, numbered title, counts line in `MailTokens.WARNING`, rich description), `text`, `table` (`MailKit` table, group rows bold, numbers right-aligned; a table with no value is not printed), `images` (half slots two per row / full alone, caption above; `imageRowHtml` writes `<!--[if mso]><img width="px"><![endif]-->` + a fluid `<!--[if !mso]>` img `max-width:px` — the contract test allows no ≥ 500 px fixed width outside MSO), `trend` ("grafikler ekteki PDF'tedir" note — never drawn in the mail), closing note (`anyRatioComputed`), button, footer. The 16-stat chain (`d.stats`) and the old badges are GONE. Images: `service/mail/TechErrorMailImages` prepares ≤ `MAX_WIDTH` 1000 × `MAX_HEIGHT` 1400 copies with `ImageAreaAverage` (a fitting PNG / JPEG is embedded AS IS — same bytes; PNG stays PNG, JPEG re-encodes at 0.9, gray PNG → sRGB like the PDF), first-fit in template slot order within `site.monitor.tech-error-report.mail-inline-mb` (6; `TECH_ERROR_REPORT_MAIL_INLINE_MB`, separate from the 15 MB PDF cap), cid `te-img-<slot>` via `EmailNotificationService.InlineImage`; a skipped (over budget / undecodable) slot shows the dashed `te-image-pdf` box "Görsel ekteki PDF'te (tam çözünürlük)" — a slot in neither list is "yüklenmedi". The sender prepares them INSIDE the `renderPdf` loader (under the PDF permit, while the images are in memory — no second DB read; the `allSkipped` fallback is captured there too because `rendered.meta()` is `withoutImages()`; a prepare failure (`mailImagePreparer` seam) falls back to it so nothing says "not uploaded") and passes `Built.inline()` to `sendHtml`; **the mail cap is the SUM** — when `pdf.length + inline bytes > max-mail-mb` the images are dropped to the all-skipped result and the PDF still goes (review 2026-10-17: a 14.9 MB PDF plus 6 MB of images bounced at the gateway AFTER the claim); `Result.unreadable` (undecodable bytes) gets a different box than `skipped` (over budget) — "okunamadı (PDF'te de çizilemedi)" vs "PDF ekinde" (`te-image-unreadable` / `te-image-pdf`); half-image pairs use a SYMMETRIC 8 px padding per cell (`halfW` 459 = 475 − 16 — a one-sided 16 px gutter overflowed Outlook's ghost cell by 8 px); section rules follow the PDF exactly (an all-exempt metrics section prints nothing, a blank textarea is not printed since release 7 (it printed "—" until then), table cells go through `TechErrorFormat.cell`, rows after a group row are indented with NBSPs) and the phone card copy of a table is added only up to `TABLE_STACK_MAX_ROWS` 12 rows (the copy of a 30 × 10 table pushed long reports toward Gmail's 102 KB clip; the test guards the TR fixture < 90 KB); `buildFull(in, prepared, appUrl, fileName)` accepts a `withoutImages()` input. Contract: `EmailResponsiveContractTest.cardIsFluidWithMsoGhost` accepts `max-width:1000px` ONLY for sample slugs starting `report-tech-error` (and requires it there); apostrophes in body text are `&#39;` (assert the plain text or the escaped form). Gates: `TechErrorReportMailTest` (layout order, sections like the PDF, images / budget / MSO widths, EN), `TechErrorMailImagesTest`, `TechErrorReportSenderTest.inlineImagesTravelWithTheMail`, `TechErrorReportPdfWriterTest` mail cases, `EmailResponsiveContractTest`, `EmailSamples` (`report-tech-error-tr` now carries two images).
    - **PO approval + executive delivery + module visibility (2026-10-17, release 6, user decisions S6: "görünürlük EKİP bazında aç/kapat (seçim yoksa herkes); onaylayıcı = ekipteki ORG ROLÜ PO olanlar (biri yeter); executive alıcıları KANAL başına liste; PO onaylayınca rapor OTOMATİK executive'e gider; red → not + taslağa döner; onaydan sonra değişirse yeniden onay"):** `tech_error_channels.executive_emails` (≤ 20 deliverable addresses, `TechErrorReportSender.parseAddresses` / `isDeliverable`; `PUT /channels/{id}` key `executive_emails` needs `canManage`; DTO `executive_emails` / `executive_count` / `approval_required` / `can_manage`). **Approval required ⇔ recipients set** (`TechErrorApprovalService.approvalRequired`; recipients removed while a request is PENDING → `decide`, in-app and via link, is refused with 409 `APPROVAL_FLOW_REMOVED`, link reason `flow_removed` (token not consumed), `approval_detail.can_decide` false and `ApprovalCard` follows the real state — badge "Onay akışı kaldırıldı" (`data-flow="removed"`, neutral tone), the old state only as an info line (`te.appr.prevState`), hint per state (`flowRemovedHintKey`: CURRENT content sent → "rapor gönderildi" + last send line — `currentContentSent` = `report.status === 'sent'` (the editor passes `reportStatus`; without it: last send after the request and not stale), never `last_sent_at` alone, which an earlier send of since-changed content also fills; an approved report also needs the send after the decision for "sent to the executives" (review 2026-10-18, round 2) — pending → "karar verilemez; alıcılar geri gelirse istek yeniden geçerli", rejected / stale / approved-unsent → "onay gerektirmiyor, doğrudan gönderilebilir" with no "pending" wording) — the team sends straight to the team address; re-adding recipients makes the pending request decidable again — review 2026-10-18): then `POST /reports/{id}/send` returns 409 `APPROVAL_REQUIRED` unless `approval_state = APPROVED ∧ approval_rev = content_rev`, and sends `Delivery.EXECUTIVE` (To = executives, CC = team address minus duplicates, log role EXECUTIVE) through the SAME send machinery (claims, queue reconcile, history) — a channel without recipients is byte-identical to before (`Delivery.TEAM`, 4-arg `send`). Report columns `approval_state` (null | PENDING | APPROVED | REJECTED) / `approval_rev` / `approval_requested_at|by_id` / `approval_decided_at|by_id` / `approval_note`, all `updatable=false`, written only by conditional JPQL (`requestApproval` binds to `content_rev` and refuses a second request for the same rev; `approve` requires PENDING ∧ `approval_rev = content_rev`; `reject` only PENDING) — `approvalDto.state` is none | pending | approved | rejected | **stale** (`content_rev > approval_rev`, request or approval). Approvers = ACTIVE users with `orgRole = PO` who are members of the team by primary OR extra membership (`AppUserRepository.findActiveByOrgRoleInTeam`; `approvers()` lists them, e-mail-less ones approve only in-app); `request` → 409 `EXEC_RECIPIENTS_MISSING` / `NO_APPROVER` / `APPROVAL_PENDING` / `REPORT_CHANGED`; `decide` → 403 for non-approvers, 409 `APPROVAL_NOT_PENDING` / `APPROVAL_STALE`, reject needs a note (`FieldValidationException` `note`, ≤ 1000). Tokens: `tech_error_approval_tokens` (one per approver with an e-mail, SHA-256 of 32 random bytes, `TOKEN_TTL` 14 d, `used_at`; a new request deletes the old rows; a decision closes all; report / channel delete removes them; retention `tech-error-approval-tokens-orphan`). Link = `GET /api/tech-error-reports/approve-link?token=` + `POST …/confirm|/reject` (`TechErrorApprovalLinkController`: server-rendered HTML like the weekly link, PUBLIC + `OriginCheckFilter` exempt, 30/min per client IP (`ClientIpResolver`, per pod — 2026-10-23 MP-33: it was the ingress address, one bucket for every PO; the decision audit IP is the client's too), `status()` reasons not_found / used / expired / not_pending / stale / approver_inactive / no_permission → `TECH_ERROR_APPROVAL_LINK_DENIED` security event; `flow_removed` is checked AFTER the person gates (a passive PO sees "approver_inactive", not "no approval needed") and is NOT a security event — neutral info audit `TECH_ERROR_APPROVAL_LINK_NOT_NEEDED` (`recordTokenAction`) + a neutral-toned page (`NEUTRAL_REASONS`, `NEUTRAL_ACCENT`; review 2026-10-18); maintenance page, decision audited `TECH_ERROR_REPORT_APPROVE|REJECT` with `via=email_token`; `decideViaToken` marks the token used BEFORE deciding — two clicks never two decisions). Mails (`TechErrorApprovalMail`, trigger `TECH_ERROR_APPROVAL` in `notification_logs`): the request mail = the full report body (`TechErrorReportMail.buildFull(…, Intro)`) with an approval card (requester, executive count, personal link, "open in Site Monitor") + the PDF, rendered ONCE under the PDF permit for all approvers (PDF busy → request stays PENDING, mails SKIPPED, `mail_problem=PDF_BUSY`, in-app approval still works; a PDF over the live limit is not attached and the body images are then checked against the limit ALONE — the body (preheader, intro, image boxes, plain-text tags, trend note) says the PDF is in Site Monitor, never "ekteki PDF"; the "all in the PDF" fallback is captured INSIDE the loader like the sender's; every request decision is a `notification_logs` row: a preparation failure writes FAILED + the class name (send-log reason INTERNAL_ERROR) for each approver not yet logged, no approver e-mail writes `SKIPPED: onaylayıcı e-postası yok` (NO_APPROVER_EMAIL), both linked — review 2026-10-18); the decision mail goes to the requester (approved + send outcome / rejected + note; 640 px). After approval the executive send runs inside `decide`; a failure keeps APPROVED and returns `sendError` (UI "Yöneticilere gönder" retries). History kinds `APPROVAL_REQUEST` / `APPROVAL` (detail state / via / note / recipients). Permission `tech_error_reports.approve` (EXECUTE; TEAM_ADMIN + USER + ADMIN) is required but not sufficient — the PO rule is checked by the service. Visibility: `site.monitor.tech-error-report.team-ids` (CSV, GLOBAL_ONLY, `TechErrorReportSettings.validate`; empty = everyone) read by `TechErrorVisibilityService` (static `current()` for the controller's static scope helpers; null bean = unrestricted): `requireRead/Write` 403 when the session has no enabled team in scope (global viewers always pass), `visibleTeamIds` / `canWrite` drop disabled teams, `/api/me/permissions` scope `channel_reports` hides the nav entry (null = shown). `GET/PUT /api/admin/tech-error-reports/settings` (`TechErrorSettingsController`: ADMIN reads, GLOBAL admin writes, audit `TECH_ERROR_SETTINGS_SAVE`; channels overview with executive / approver counts and the period report state) → Settings → Platform → Teknik hata raporu (`?sec=techerror`, `TechErrorReportSettings.jsx`, `MultiTeamSelect`, read-only for a scoped admin; group `tech-error-report` skipped on the general page). UI: `ApprovalCard` (state badge, who / when, note, approvers; PO Approve / Reject dialog with the note field; data-slot `te-approval`, `data-state`), action bar `te-approval-request-bar` ("Onaya gönder") / `te-send-exec` ("Yöneticilere gönder") replace `te-send` on approval channels; `ChannelDialog` executive recipients field (managers only, client regex = server rule); channel card chip `te-channel-approval`; history rows. Gates: `TechErrorApprovalServiceTest` (H2: transitions, stale, tokens, approvers, mails, PDF busy), `TechErrorVisibilityTest`, `TechErrorApprovalLinkControllerTest`, `TechErrorApprovalMailTest`, `TechErrorReportControllerTest.approvalRequest|approvalDecide|sendRequiresApproval|channelExecutiveEmails|reportApprovalDetail|moduleVisibility`, `AuthControllerTest.myPermissions_carriesScope` (channel_reports), `EmailSamples` (`report-tech-error-approval-tr`, `report-tech-approval-approved|rejected-tr`), `TechErrorApproval.test.jsx`, `TechErrorReportSettings.test.jsx`, `NavChannelReportsGate.test.jsx`, `e2e/channel-reports.spec.js` ("PO onayı", 390/768/1280). **Review round 1 (2026-10-17):** `requestApproval` / `approve` compare `COALESCE(content_rev, 0)` (rows from before the counter are NULL — `touchContent` already coalesces); a PENDING request older than `RESEND_AFTER` 10 min may be requested again (`resend` flag → "Onay isteğini yeniden gönder": reminder, and the way out of a request interrupted after its UPDATE), an APPROVED unchanged report is never re-requested; the sender's EXECUTIVE path re-checks APPROVED ∧ `approval_rev = content_rev` on the POST-claim read and otherwise releases the claim with `SKIPPED: onay eskidi …` + 409 `APPROVAL_STALE` (history reason `APPROVAL_STALE`) — an autosave between the approval and the send can't mail content the PO never saw; `decideViaToken` validates the note BEFORE `markUsed`, refuses approve on a stale request without consuming the token (the link page then offers reject only — `TokenStatus.stale`), and restores `used_at` when `decide` throws anything but a `CodedConflictException`; the request mail without a fitting PDF says so (`buildFull(fileName = null)`, `mail_problem = PDF_TOO_LARGE`), the request history row is written AFTER the mails with the real recipients and the problem code; a losing race on the link (`APPROVAL_NOT_PENDING`) is an action audit, not a security event; `approvalDetail` runs the approver query once; `last_sent_to` holds the To list only (the team CC is in `notification_logs.cc`). **Rollback note:** a pre-release-6 pod ignores the approval columns and sends to the team address on `/send` (no approval gate) and does not serve the link paths; the settings key is unknown there (module visible to everyone). Avoid approving during the rollout.
    - **Counts back in "Verileri" (2026-10-17, release 7, user decision: "Yönetici Özeti sayfasındaki kota ve map hataları kısımlarını çıkarıp X Kanalı — Eylül 2026 Verileri kısmında bu değerlerin girişini yapalım"):** the default template has NO hosted sections any more — `unmapped-count` / `unmapped-note` / `quota-count` / `quota-note` (same ids, labels and types) sit in the `summary` ("Verileri") metrics section right after `error-count` (8 sections: exec-summary, summary, rates, issues, sla-shots, trend-note, charts, charts-daily); summary sentences, tiles, rates, trend, history and the `host` capability itself are unchanged (a team may still embed a section with "Gösterim yeri"; the note then prints under the sentence as before, otherwise as a textarea of the Verileri section). One-time `TechErrorCountsInFiguresMigration` (KEY `2026-10-17-tech-error-counts-in-figures` — never rename after release; waits for the SLA-tiles marker (chain calc → embed → SLA → counts); 10 min after boot (the rollout window — see the rollback note), hourly, `scheduler_lock` `tech-error-counts-in-figures-migration`, ≤ 24 attempts per pod, marker re-read inside the lock and written only after ALL channels; `saveTemplate(…, null, "SiteMonitor")` conditional, ≤ 3 retries): a HOSTED `unmapped` / `quota` section that is EXACTLY the 4a default (title TR / EN, count `number` + note `textarea` with the default labels, no hint / link / calc / target / direction; any host id) is dissolved into the `summary` metrics section after `error-count` (map first, then quota, end of the list when `error-count` is gone) and the section is removed; skipped when there is no SEPARATE (un-hosted) `summary` metrics section, the section was changed, the team un-hosted it, or the 30-field cap would be exceeded; values survive by id (`carry` on "apply latest template": `lost_values` 0, rates recomputed). Test oracle: `TechErrorTestTemplates.hostedDefaults(lang)` = the 4a / 4b layout (written titles — a real oracle for the old-shape match); the 4a embed and 4b tiles migrations now produce THAT layout and the chain `… → TechErrorCountsInFiguresMigration.migrate` must reproduce today's default byte-identically (`TechErrorSummaryEmbedMigrationTest.bothShapesBecomeToday`, `TechErrorSlaTilesMigrationTest.before4bBecomesToday` derive `before4b` from the hosted layout); `TechErrorTemplateTest.hostStrictAndLenient` and `TechErrorReportPdfWriterTest.summaryEmbeddedNotesAndFacts` run on it (the host feature stays tested). **Blank notes (review 2026-10-17):** a BLANK `textarea` of a metrics section is NOT printed in the PDF or the mail (`TechErrorReportPdfWriter.blankText`; an all-blank-note section prints no title) — the same rule the hosted notes had; a blank short `text` field keeps "—". On screen the generic metrics layout still draws the two notes AFTER the compact fields (grid, then long texts), not literally after `error-count`. Rollback note: a pre-release-7 pod shows the same template (plain metrics fields) — nothing is lost; the migrated layout stays; a report created on an old pod during the rollout copies the hosted layout and takes the new one with "apply latest template"; a CHANNEL created on an old pod AFTER the marker was written keeps the hosted layout (one pass, marker inside the lock) — "varsayılana dön" / un-embed in the template editor. Gates: `TechErrorCountsInFiguresMigrationTest` (pure rules, waits for the SLA marker, H2 job + refresh keeps values), `TechErrorTemplateTest.defaultLayoutMatchesTheRequest`, `TechErrorReportControllerTest` (8 default sections), `TechErrorValuesTest.lenientStored` / `TechErrorReportServiceTest.refreshTemplate` (field removed from Verileri, not a section), `TechErrorReportPdfWriterTest.blankTextareaNotPrinted`.

Filters are component state (no URL keys).     - **Channel order + period summary + team badge (2026-10-17/18, user-approved B7 / B8 + user report "takım bilgisinin yanında 2 ikon var; takıma tıklanmıyor"):** sort option `order` in `channelListModel.CHANNEL_SORTS` = team A→Z (Turkish collation, `team_id` tie so a team stays contiguous) → `sort_order` ascending (null last, 0 valid) → name; the other sorts are byte-identical. In that sort the row / card `KebabMenu` carries "Yukarı taşı" / "Aşağı taşı" (`KebabMenu` items accept `disabled` + `ariaLabel`; accessible name "Yukarı taşı: <kanal>") ONLY for `can_edit ∧ ¬shared_access`, disabled at the team's first / last position, while busy, and while a search or status / KPI filter is active (`te.sort.orderLocked` — the neighbour could be hidden; the team filter is server-side and does not disable it); the hint `te-sort-hint` shows only while a visible row is movable. A move sends the team's FULL id order in ONE request: `PUT /api/tech-error-reports/teams/{teamId}/channel-order {ids}` (gate = the channel PUT's own-scope rule `requireWrite` + `canWriteOwn` — a grant → 403, AUDIT → 403; unreadable team → 404 `TEAM_NOT_FOUND`; missing / extra / foreign / duplicate ids → 400 `VALIDATION_FAILED` `ids`); `TechErrorReportService.reorderChannels` writes 1…n in one transaction with a targeted `updateSortOrder` only where the value changes, re-reads the team's orders (a concurrent write leaving a mixed order → 409 `CONCURRENT_MODIFICATION`, rolled back) and writes ONE audit `TECH_ERROR_CHANNEL_REORDER` (`team_id`, `before` / `after` comma-joined ids — AuditDiff truncates arrays at 20) only when something changed. The client falls back to per-channel PUTs (`reorderPlan`: a swap only when every channel of the team has a filled, distinct `sort_order`, otherwise 1…n renumbering) only on 405 or a 404 without `TEAM_NOT_FOUND` (old pod during a rollout); a fallback failure after ≥ 1 saved PUT says `te.order.partial` and shows the server's order. A move bumps the load sequence (late loads dropped), stays busy until its reload settles (owner-keyed busy flags — toggle / delete / start-report never clear it; Refresh disabled), announces the result in `te-order-status` (`aria-live`) and the pager follows the moved row. A new channel gets max(team) + 1 (1 in an empty team; null while the team still has unordered channels). All `TECH_ERROR_*` audit events are in `auditFormat` SPECIAL now (they showed raw names). Kişisel Panom colour panel `stat_channel_reports_missing` (`statDefs`, `preset: false` — presets / default layout unchanged) needs `tech_error_reports.read` + the pseudo-permission `@channel_reports` (`scope.channel_reports !== false`, the nav rule) and reads `GET /api/tech-error-reports/period-summary` → `{period:{year,month}, active, sent, open, missing, inactive}` (same read scope / module visibility / 403 as the overview, same meaning as `channelListModel.channelCounts` via `TechErrorReportService.reportStatus`, no template parsing, no `reconcileQueued`, memoized per scope key for `site.monitor.tech-error-report.summary-cache-ms` 30000, ≤ 500 keys; old pod 404 / 405 → the overview); value = active channels whose period report is missing, 0 green / > 0 amber (a task, not an outage), 403 → grey "no access", no list / no active channel → grey, click → `channelreports` `te_st=missing`. **Team badge:** the channel row / card, channel header, report header and Settings rows draw ONE icon (TeamBadge's own; the extra `Users` icon is gone) and the badge opens the team members window — except on rows / headers / reports seen only through an access grant (`shared_access` → static: the members dialog would show the owner team's people); on touch the name and the team line are 12 px apart (`pointer-coarse:gap-3`, shared `ChannelNameAndTeam`) so the badge's `::after` hit area never overlaps the name button; the Settings row badge widens its touch area (`pointer-coarse:after:-inset-y-3`); the e2e touch helpers count an absolutely positioned `::after` and `badgeHitOverlaps` checks the overlap.
