    - **PDF running header, table of contents, bookmarks + template preview PDF (2026-10-17/18, user-approved C15 / D18 + review):** every page except the cover carries a running header ("<kanal> · <ay yıl>" in the template language left; the resolved report title right when it fits — a fitting title of any length is printed, "too short, omit" applies only when it had to be shortened) stamped with the page numbers at the end (`PdfCanvas.setRunningHeader`; continuation content starts `HEADER_BAND` 10 pt lower; other PDFs never call it and stay byte-identical). The PDF is rendered in TWO passes (`TechErrorReportPdfWriter.write`): pass 1 is layout only (`layoutOnly`, no image embedding — layout depends on image size, never bytes); when the natural layout has ≥ `TOC_MIN_PAGES` (4) pages, pass 2 reserves the "İçindekiler" / "Contents" block on PAGE 1 under the cover band (and the sample banner) and content starts on a fresh page 2 (entries that don't fit continue at the top of page 2, content then on page 3); numbers, dot leaders and invisible links are filled AFTER the content through `PdfCanvas.drawOn(page)` from pass 2's own section marks (matched by title in order) — no page is ever inserted, so the TOC never splits a section and its numbers are the final pagination. Only PRINTED sections are listed (≤ 2 lines, then "…"). Every `EMBED_SCALES` step renders both passes. Bookmarks (`PdfCanvas.outline`) for every printed section at any length; document title = the resolved report title (`DisplayDocTitle`). Template preview: `POST /api/tech-error-reports/channels/{id}/template/preview-pdf {template}` (gate = reading the template, AUDIT and grant-only readers included; strict validation → 400 `VALIDATION_FAILED` `template`; shared PDF permits → 429; at most 6 per user per minute per pod, counted after validation → 429 `RATE_LIMITED` + `Retry-After` + `retry_after_seconds`; nothing written, inline + `no-store`, `<slug>-sablon-onizleme.pdf` / `-template-preview.pdf`) renders `TechErrorSampleValues.previewInput` (pure, deterministic: counts from the id hash, denominator-only roots 1.0–1.5 M, ratio numerators ≤ denominator / 4, `minus` ≤ numerator / 2 in ≤ 40 shrinking passes, computed fields via `TechErrorCalc.apply` and any percent / yes-no still empty (broken / manual formula) gets a sample value, last month 2–8 % lower for `{d:}` / tile deltas, every yes/no "Yes" so nothing is exempt, no images, empty trend, `Provenance` null, preparedBy = session `displayName`) with `TechErrorPdfInput.sample = true` → marked on EVERY page (page-1 banner "ÖRNEK ÖNİZLEME — …", running header "ÖRNEK ÖNİZLEME · …" / "SAMPLE PREVIEW · …", cover "Hazırlayan: <name> (örnek önizleme)", document title "… — örnek önizleme"). UI: template toolbar "Örnek PDF" (`te-tpl-preview-pdf`) sends the CURRENT draft (unsaved edits), opens the tab on click (blocked → download + `te.tpl.previewPdfDownloaded`); only an EDITOR is stopped by client-side field errors (`te.tpl.previewFixErrors`), a read-only viewer always sends and sees the server's message; the blob URL is released after `PREVIEW_URL_TTL_MS` (10 min) or when the editor unmounts. Tests that look for a title's page skip the contents page. Gates: `TechErrorReportPdfWriterTest.runningHeaderOnContinuationPages|tableOfContentsAndBookmarks|tocNeverSplitsASection|shortReportHasBookmarksButNoToc|tableOfContentsEnglish|shortTitleShowsInHeader|documentTitle|sampleBannerOnFirstPage`, `TechErrorSampleValuesTest`, `TechErrorReportControllerTest.templatePreviewPdf|templatePreviewPdfRateLimited`, `WeeklyOutagePdfWriterTest` / `ExecutiveSummaryPdfWriterTest` (shared `PdfCanvas`), `TechErrorTemplatePreviewPdf.test.jsx`, `e2e/channel-reports.spec.js` (template test: preview step).
    - **Reminders + live mail limits (2026-10-17, release 12, user decisions C9 / C10 / E21; review 2026-10-18):**
      - **Schedule:** `TechErrorReminderService` runs daily at 09:15 Istanbul (`site.monitor.tech-error-report.reminder-cron`, `…reminder-enabled`) under `scheduler_lock` `tech-error-reminder` PLUS a cluster round claim `round:tech-error-reminder` (`ClusterScheduleService.claim`; one row, due = next Istanbul midnight → one round per cluster per day; a claim read failure runs the round, and the per-row claims still prevent duplicates; `run()` has no round gate).
      - **Period reminder:** only teams in `…reminder-team-ids` (empty = OFF). It starts on `reminder-day` (1–28, 5) and repeats every `reminder-repeat-days` (0–14, 3; 0 = once) for the previous month. It covers ACTIVE channels created BEFORE the period end (1st of the next month 00:00 Istanbul) whose report is missing or never sent (`last_sent_at` null). The pass starts with `reconcileQueued`. A QUEUED send is waited for while its result can still land (`QUEUED_RESULT_WINDOW`); an older one is listed as "Gönderim sonucu alınamadı" (`State.QUEUED_UNKNOWN`). On a channel WITHOUT an approval flow (`approvalRequired` false) the report's old approval state is ignored — listed as a draft ("Taslak — gönderilmedi"; `stateOf(row, now, approvalFlow)`), never "waiting for PO approval" while the approval reminder skips it. One mail per team, subject `[Site Monitor] Teknik hata raporu hatırlatması — <period> · <team>` (the 421 write-back matches subject AND recipient — `EmailNotificationService.retryRowFor`, newest of the 20 queued rows with that subject whose recipient list holds a To address; otherwise only the newest row whose recipient column can't be compared (no `@` — group name, blank); a row with addresses that don't match is never written (nothing written, one WARN); To unreadable → the old newest-by-subject rule). The team address goes to at most 20 recipients (`parseAddressesDetailed`); the overflow is never silent (" · +N adres sınır dışında" + WARN). `email_status` carries ONLY the mail service's answer (SMTP log kind / SENT counts); the push note (queued count, the service reason or "N alıcı atlandı") and the overflow note go into `message` (`withNote` — the note is never clipped). Optional team push (`reminder-push`).
      - **Approval reminder:** a PENDING, non-stale request whose Istanbul request DATE + `approval-reminder-days` (1–14, 3) ≤ today (`firstDue` — the first reminder counts days like the repeats, not 24-hour steps; review 2026-10-18) gets one short mail per PO with an e-mail, every N days per request (`ref_key` = request time). The body's "N gündür bekliyor" counts the same Istanbul days (`waitingDays`; a 16:00 request reminded next day at 09:15 says "1 gündür", never "0"). The subject carries channel + period (`TechErrorReminderMail.uniqueTitle`). No new token: the original link is mentioned only for a live token (unused, not superseded, unexpired, same `approval_rev`) with `tech_error_approval_tokens.mailed_at` set. `request()` sets `mailed_at` only when that PO's mail returned SENT / QUEUED AND carried the link (base-url set); otherwise the mail says "open it in Site Monitor". Every token this release creates carries `mail_tracked = TRUE` (patch line; the producer stamps `mailed_at`): an unstamped TRUE token really didn't reach the PO (old "expired or not sent to you" text). A valid unstamped token with `mail_tracked` NULL (made by an older release, or by an old pod during a rolling deploy / rollback) is "unconfirmed" (`links().unconfirmed()`): the mail neither claims the link expired nor that it wasn't sent — it points to Site Monitor and says the original link may still work. The split comes from the token's own data, never a calendar date (review 2026-10-18: the hard-coded 2026-10-19 cut-off broke on a manual prod deploy).
      - **Claims:** `tech_error_reminders` (channel × period × kind, UNIQUE) is claimed BEFORE sending (insert, then a `claim_rev` CAS). If nothing was delivered (mail FAILED, no push) the claim is released; a SKIPPED mail (no address / channel off) is final for the window.
      - **Gates before the claim:** module hidden from the team, passive team, and (approval only) approval flow removed (`approvalRequired` false). A refusal never consumes the claim: a gate row `SKIP:HIDDEN|PASSIVE` / `ASKIP:HIDDEN|PASSIVE|NOFLOW` writes the SKIPPED log once per channel × period × reason, and the reminder goes normally once the gate opens. System-maintenance mute → SKIPPED row, no claim.
      - **Pruning:** rows not updated for 6 months are pruned every run — by ROW age, never by period age (by period, a pending approval older than 6 months was reminded daily).
      - **Logging:** every decision is a `notification_logs` row (`TECH_ERROR_REMINDER` / `TECH_ERROR_APPROVAL_REMINDER`, alertEventId 0).
      - **Other approvers informed:** after a decision, POs who did not decide (not the decider, not the requester, not a shared mailbox) get "no action needed" (role `APPROVER_INFO`). It is sent on `loginIssueMailExecutor` after the decision is committed (`TechErrorApprovalService.afterDecision`: afterCommit when a transaction is active; caller thread when there is no pool or it rejects).
      - **Live mail limits (E21):** `max-mail-mb` (5–50, 15) and `mail-inline-mb` (0–20, 6, must be below the limit) are GLOBAL_ONLY settings, read live by the sender, the PDF ladder and the approval mail (`TechErrorReportSettings.liveInt`; the env value is only the boot fallback). One read per send / preview / test / approval mail, passed to `TechErrorReportService.renderPdf(loader, limitBytes)` so the PDF ladder and the "fits?" check use the SAME value (`renderPdf(loader)` = download, reads once); `Rendering.maxMailMb` carries it to the `PDF_TOO_LARGE` text. An out-of-range value — stored override or boot / env value — falls back to the catalogue default, the value the screen shows (`liveInt` = `effectiveInt` rule; review 2026-10-18). The fallback is never silent: one WARN per key per pod (`TechErrorReportSettings.warnOutOfRange`: key, value, source, default used) and `GET …/settings/reminders` returns `out_of_range {<field>: {source: env|stored, value, default}}` (`outOfRange`, in-memory reads only), shown as a note in the field's hint (`tes-rem-oor-*`; `te.rem.outOfRangeEnv|Stored` for a writer, `…ReadOnly` "a global admin can save a valid value" for a read-only viewer). Such a field is a pending REPAIR (`isRepairKey` / `repairKeys`, review 2026-10-18 round 2): the form holds the effective (= default) value, which `changedFields` / `isFormDirty` / `validateForm` count as changed, so ONE save writes it (the server stores it as an override even when it equals the default — the env value then no longer applies, a broken stored value is overwritten, the note disappears; `AppSettingsServiceTest.techErrorOutOfRangeEnv_savedDefaultIsStored`); the save bar is enabled with `te.rem.repairPending` and no Discard, while the page's leave prompt and the Refresh draft rule read only the user's own changes (`{ repairs: false }`).
      - **API / UI:** `GET/PUT /api/admin/tech-error-reports/settings/reminders` — ADMIN reads (with `read_only`), GLOBAL admin writes, scoped admin 403; only sent keys are written and `team_ids` is ignored there; field errors 400 + `field`; a team id that is ALREADY stored but whose team was deleted is dropped silently on save (only a NEWLY added unknown id is 400) — on both endpoints, except that a visibility list made only of deleted teams is kept (an empty list would mean "everyone"); the UI shows such ids as "Silinmiş takım" (directory unreadable: "Takım adı okunamadı"), never a bare `#id` (`teamOptions(…, missingLabel)`, `missingTeamText`); several of them are numbered ("Silinmiş takım 1", "Silinmiş takım 2" / "Adı okunamayan takım N") from ONE page-level map (`missingTeamOrdinals` over the union of the SAVED visibility + reminder ids, ascending; draft-only ids after them; `missingTeamLabelFor` ignores the picker's own `missingLabel(id, ordinal, count)` numbers — review round 2: a team kept a different number in each picker and the numbers shifted when a chip was removed), and a stored visibility list made ONLY of deleted teams shows a warning banner (`tes-only-deleted`, `onlyDeletedTeams`: only global viewers see the module now — select teams or clear the list; no banner while the directory is unreadable); an empty body writes nothing and audits nothing; audit `TECH_ERROR_SETTINGS_SAVE` + `keys`. `GET/PUT /settings` stay byte-identical to release 11 (only `team_ids`, missing = empty).
      - **Rolling deploy:** the card never PUTs `/settings`. An old pod answers the new path 404 / 405 and writes nothing → the card shows `te.rem.unsupported`, turns read-only and keeps the draft (`reminderState`, `isUnsupportedResponse`).
      - **Card:** `TechErrorReminderSettings` (`onSaving` → the page disables Refresh AND every "Tekrar dene" button and bumps `loadSeq` at save start; `onSaved`; `onUnsupported`). Only CHANGED fields are validated (`validateForm(form, limits, saved)`, rule 9). A failed read keeps the last values and never invents them. `FlowBadges` shows an unknown executive / PO count as "—", never "PO yok".
      - **Gates:** `TechErrorReminderServiceTest` (incl. `oldPeriodApprovalOncePerWindow`, `channelCreatedAfterPeriodIsNotReminded`, `stuckQueuedIsReminded`, `dailyRoundOncePerCluster`, `approvalGates`, `moduleHiddenIsSkipped`, `passiveTeamIsSkippedBeforeClaim`, `subjectsAreUnique`, `pushNoteTellsTheReason`, the superseded token in `approvalReminderOncePerWindow`), `TechErrorApprovalServiceTest.tokenMailedAtOnlyWhenTheLinkReachedThePo|otherApproversInformedInBackgroundAfterCommit|otherApproversInformed`, review 2026-10-18: `TechErrorReminderServiceTest.noApprovalFlowMeansDraft|periodReminderOverflowIsLogged|firstApprovalReminderUsesIstanbulDays|unmailedTokenAfterStampSaysNotSent|waitingDaysCountIstanbulDays|untrackedTokenIsNeutralWhateverTheDate`, `TechErrorReportSettingsTest.outOfRangeIsWarnedOnceAndReported`, `TechErrorSettingsControllerTest.getRemindersReportsOutOfRangeValues`, `TechErrorApprovalLinkControllerTest.flowRemovedIsNeutral`, `SmtpLogQueryServiceTest.reminderRowsAreNotResendable`, `EmailNotificationServiceTest.retryWriteBackNeverHitsAnotherRecipient`, `TechErrorApprovalServiceTest.decideRefusedWhenFlowRemoved|requestWithoutPdfKeepsImagesThatFitAlone|everyRequestDecisionLeavesALogRow`, `TechErrorReportMailTest.noAttachmentNeverClaimsAttachedPdf`, `TechErrorReportSenderTest.mailLimitIsReadOncePerRender`, `TechErrorPdfSizeLadderTest`, `EmailNotificationServiceTest.retryWriteBackMatchesRecipient`, `TechErrorSettingsControllerTest.deletedStoredTeamIsDroppedSilently`, `TechErrorSettingsControllerTest` (release-11 contract + reminders endpoint), `TechErrorReportSettingsTest`, `AppSettingsServiceTest`, `EmailSamples` (`report-tech-reminder-tr|en`, `report-tech-approval-reminder-tr`, `report-tech-approval-info-tr`), `techErrorReminderModel.test.js`, `TechErrorReportSettings.test.jsx`, `e2e/settings-techerror.spec.js`.
      - **Two-phase claim (multi-pod R2a-D1, MP-41):** the claim (INSERT / CAS) writes `tech_error_reminders.inflight_at` (= `updated_at`; column via `ReportIntegritySchemaPatch`); every decision clears it conditionally (`finishInflight`: sent / final SKIPPED / given up; `release` clears it too). A pending stamp older than `INFLIGHT_STALE` (10 min) means the claiming pod died between claim and decision: the reminder is due again (also with repeat 0 — it used to be lost for that period for good) on the next daily round and one `SKIPPED: önceki deneme yarıda kaldı — yeniden deneniyor` row is written (`ST_RETAKEN`). At least once: a pod that died after SMTP sends one duplicate. An old (20.165.1) pod's claim / release moves `updated_at` past the stamp and voids it (`inflightPending`: `inflight_at >= updated_at`). The `ST_RETAKEN` row maps to send-log reason `RETRIED_AFTER_INTERRUPT` (review REP-5; it showed `SKIPPED_OTHER`). Gates: `TechErrorReminderServiceTest.claimedThenPodDied_repeatZero_remindedNextDay|inflightClearedOnEveryDecision|oldPodClaimVoidsInflight|approvalClaimedThenPodDied_retakenNextDay|dueWithInflight`.
      - **Known limit:** channels whose repeat windows drift apart get separate mails on different days.
    - **Push notifications (2026-10-18, user request: "teknik hata raporu ile ilgili bildirimleri haftalık rapordaki gibi push bildirimleri ile de yapalım. ayarlar üzerinden kontrol edilebilsin"):**
      - **Service:** `TechErrorPushService` mirrors the weekly report push (`WeeklyReportService.pushTeamApproval`): direct push `UserPushService.enqueueDirectLocalized` (opt-out, hourly cap, circuit breaker, outbox dedupe) or team notice `enqueueTeamNoticeLocalized` (role group / min level / passive / personal prefs in the resolver, exclusion set). Level WARNING for every event (weekly precedent: information, not an alarm — the failure is said in the text). `monitorType` `TECH_ERROR_REPORT` (push log / "my pushes"); the personal FAMILY filter (monitor families) does not apply, exactly like WEEKLY_REPORT. Direct recipients now carry an optional `DirectRecipient.skipReason` = `UserPushRecipientResolver.preferenceSkip` (min level / snooze → `SKIPPED_USER_LEVEL` / `SKIPPED_USER_SNOOZE` rows); the 3/4-arg constructors give null (weekly unchanged). Texts TR + EN per recipient `push_lang`, `PushText.pushSafe`-invariant, no links (`TechErrorPushServiceTest.textsArePushSafe`).
      - **Events (trigger · recipients · dedupe key):** approval request `TECH_ERROR_APPROVAL` → the request mail's approvers (`approvers()`, e-mail-less POs included, requester never; a shared-mailbox PO counts as reached by ADDRESS), text says "the approval e-mail did not reach you" for POs whose mail wasn't SENT/QUEUED · `te-appr:<report>:<requestedAt ms>`, a resend of a pending request (> 10 min, same rev) `te-appr-resend:…` + "Reminder:" text. Approval reminder `TECH_ERROR_APPROVAL_REMINDER` → all approvers (requester never) inside the job's claim/window · `te-appr-remind:<report>:<requestedAt ms>:r<send_count>` on the first try of a round and `…:r<send_count>t<try>` on its retries (never the day; review round 3: one key per round let the outbox dedupe swallow tries 2–3 for a PO whose first push was RATE_LIMITED / snoozed — a retry lists only POs not settled yet, so a reached PO never gets a second push; gate `retryPushIsNotBlockedByTheOutboxDedupe` with the REAL `UserPushService` + repository); the claim is decided PER PO and per ROUND (review 2026-10-18, rounds 1–2): a PO is reached when their mail was SENT/QUEUED or their own push was queued (`approvalReminder` returns the approver ids from the additive `enqueueDirectLocalized` key `queued_usernames`; a result without it counts only "everyone queued"), a SKIPPED mail (no address / channel off / passive) is final for that PO for the round — it goes into the same settled set, so a retry neither mails, pushes nor logs it again (`skippedPoIsFinalForTheRound`). When an e-mail PO was reached by neither, the claim is released through the SAME conditional UPDATE that writes `tech_error_reminders.reached_ids` = `<ref>#<send_count>#<try>:<comma ids>` (patch line; `updatable=false`, only `release` writes it; `RoundState`, ≤ 4000 chars). A retry of the same round (same ref + `send_count` — release decrements it, the next claim increments it back) sends NEITHER mail NOR push to the stored POs and counts them as reached; after `MAX_TRIES` (3) failed tries the round gives up: the claim is kept and one `notification_logs` row with the missed addresses — `SKIPPED: hatırlatma 3 denemede ulaşmadı — N onaylayıcı` when the last try ended with the mail server refusing (address / delivery problem, send-log reason `RETRIES_EXHAUSTED`) or `FAILED: hatırlatma 3 denemede hatayla bitti …` when it ended with an application error (exception, no result from the mail service — `internalFailure` = `reasonOf` INTERNAL_ERROR; FAILED class, reason `RETRIES_FAILED`; `errorGiveUpIsFailedAndSeparate`); the next repeat window starts a fresh round. An unexpected exception counts as a try too. The period reminder (one team mailbox + the team push; push queued counts as delivered) uses the same 3-tries-per-round cap (`retryOrGiveUpPeriod`, try count in `reached_ids` with no ids) — a permanently bouncing team address was retried every day without end. With repeat 0 ("send once") a period reminder whose team address keeps failing now stops FOR GOOD for that period after 3 failing days (`periodRepeatZeroStopsForGoodAfterThreeFailures`); the give-up is in `notification_logs` (SMTP log). The period reminder covers several channels, so it is not linked to a channel and does not appear in the channel send log; the approval reminder's give-up row is linked (channel send log, reasons above). Gates: `TechErrorReminderServiceTest.approvalReminderDecidesPerPo` (days 1–4 + next round: reached POs exactly once per round, the bouncing PO 3 times then SKIPPED), `periodReminderRetriesAreCapped`, `roundStateFormat`. The PO mail rows carry ` · push N`. Decision `TECH_ERROR_DECISION` → the requester (active; skipped when they decided it themselves; ALSO when they have since left the team — they asked for the approval, and the module's direct pushes check the account only, never team membership; same for the direct delivered push below) with the executive send outcome — one sentence per outcome, never a raw code (`approvedOutcome`: SENT / QUEUED / SKIPPED = channel off or no active recipient, not a failure / APPROVAL_STALE = changed after the approval, submit again / SEND_IN_PROGRESS = already being sent, result on the report / PDF_BUSY = send again shortly / PDF_TOO_LARGE / anything else "did not reach the executives, send again") — or, on a rejection, only core "reddedildi." / "was rejected." + advice "Ret notu Site Monitor'de." / "See note in Site Monitor." (`REJECT_CORE_*` / `REJECT_ADVICE_*`) · `te-decision:<report>:<decidedAt ms>`. The rejection NOTE is never put in a push (decision 2026-10-18: the push app is outside the company network, so no free text goes there — the link scrubber of the earlier review rounds was removed with it). Texts are fitted to the recipient's LIVE push limit (`UserPushService.messageCharLimit`, 80–320; `fitText`): parts head + subject (channel + period) + core sentence + advice + by-line; drop order by-line (full → short name = AD display name without the "(…)" part, ≤ 40 → none) → the channel name shrinks (period kept, to keep the advice) → the advice goes (`approvedParts` = [core, advice]); head, period and core never drop (`textsFitTheConfiguredLimit`: every outcome + rejection, 66-char channel, 71-char AD name, TR + EN at 80 / 120 / 200 / 320; `decisionUsesTheLiveLimit`). Review 2026-10-19: every core fits the MINIMUM limit (`MIN_MESSAGE_LIMIT` 80) with the longest period ("Ağustos 2026" / "September 2026") — the detail moved into the advice (shown at wider limits, dropped first), e.g. "onaylandı; PDF sırası dolu, gitmedi." + "Yöneticilere henüz gönderilmedi; birazdan …" (gate `outcomeTextsFitTheMinimumLimit`: all 8 outcomes + unknown code, rejection and the requester's delivered text, TR + EN, all 12 months, no / short / 66-char channel). Delivered `TECH_ERROR_SENT` → team notice minus the sender and minus `alsoNotified` (decide passes the requester to the new 7-arg `TechErrorReportSender.send(…, extraCc, alsoNotified)` only when the decision push goes to them) · `te-sent:<report>:<sent_rev>:<claim ms>` — written only after the conditional `finishSent` won; QUEUED pushes nothing until `reconcileQueued` turns it SENT (`pushQueuedResult`, after the REQUIRES_NEW commit, same key → never twice; the key fields come from the row `finishQueuedWithEvent` read INSIDE its transaction (`QueuedFinish` / `QueuedRow`) — never re-read after the commit, where a new claim could have changed `send_claimed_at`; role from the log row: EXECUTIVE → "yöneticilere"). A QUEUED send keeps its `alsoNotified` with the queue (final review 2026-10-18 — the requester got the decision push "queued" and then the team "delivered" push for the same delivery): `finishQueuedNotified` writes `tech_error_reports.queued_notified_id` (patch line, `updatable=false`; the plain `finishQueued` clears it, so a later queued claim on the row never inherits it) and `pushQueuedResult` passes it as `alsoNotified` — the requester and the deciding PO (the sender) are left out of that delivery's team push; a requester who got no decision push (decision push off, own decision) still gets it. That requester gets a DIRECT delivered push instead (user decision 2026-10-19, "gitsin" — the decision push said "queued" and they never learned the delivery): `pushQueuedResult` calls `TechErrorPushService.queuedDeliveredToRequester` BEFORE the team push (trigger `TECH_ERROR_SENT`, gated by the `delivered` key, `requesterDeliveredText` core "… teslim edildi." + advice "Kuyruktaki gönderim tamamlandı." + "Onaylayan:", fitted to the live push limit with `fitText` (the core fits 80), no links; the requester gets it even after leaving the team; key = the team push's `te-sent:<report>:<sent_rev>:<claim ms>` (`sentKey`) so the outbox key × user dedupe never lets two pods / repeated reads / the team path push that person twice; requester = sender or passive → nothing, opt-out / level / snooze → their skip rows) — one push per person; a queued FAILURE is unchanged (`sendFailedTo` already tells the requester). Rolling deploy: an old pod's `finishQueued` does not clear the column, so a later queued send on the same row during the rollout can leave that one requester out of its team push (they get the direct delivered push instead; an old pod reconciling sends neither) (gates `TechErrorPushServiceTest.queuedApprovalDeliveryIsNotPushedTwice`, `TechErrorReportServiceTest.queuedDeliveryExcludesTheAlreadyNotifiedRequester`, `TechErrorReportSenderTest.queuedKeepsTheAlreadyNotifiedPerson`). Not sent `TECH_ERROR_SEND_FAILED` → the sender: FAILED written by this claim (reason PDF_TOO_LARGE named), a queued send reconciled FAILED (`QUEUE_FAILED`, `te-fail:<report>:<claim ms>`; on EXECUTIVE delivery also the approval requester — `sendFailedTo`, ids deduplicated, so a requester who queued it gets one push), and a superseded old queue whose owner ≠ current sender when this attempt didn't deliver either (`UNKNOWN`, `…:unknown`). Released claims (PDF busy, approval stale), kept queues and the test mail push nothing.
      - **Gates:** each event has its own GLOBAL_ONLY BOOL key, default ON: `site.monitor.tech-error-report.push.approval-request|approval-reminder|decision|delivered|failed` (`TechErrorReportSettings.PUSH_KEYS`, `pushOn` — unreadable = on); the period reminder push keeps `reminder-push`. Module hidden for the team → no push; system-maintenance mute: reminders keep their own mute (no claim), report pushes behave like weekly (not muted). Every method swallows its own failure (WARN) — send / approval / reconcile results never change.
      - **Settings:** `GET/PUT /api/admin/tech-error-reports/settings/push` (`TechErrorSettingsController.PUSH_FIELDS`: `period_reminder` → `reminder-push`, `approval_request`, `approval_reminder`, `decision`, `delivered`, `failed`; + `push_enabled` = global channel): only sent keys written, non-boolean → 400 `VALIDATION_FAILED` + `field`, empty body writes / audits nothing, audit `TECH_ERROR_SETTINGS_SAVE` + `keys`; scoped admin reads (`read_only`), 403 on PUT. UI `TechErrorPushSettings` (`tes-push`, model `techErrorPushModel.js`, Java field order read by the test): three groups (period / PO approval / sending result), shadcn Switch rows with descriptions + HelpTip, "push channel off" banner → Settings → Push, old pod 404/405 → `te.push.unsupported` read-only, draft kept. The period reminder's checkbox left the reminders card (`tes-rem-push-moved` note; `reminder_push` is no longer in that card's form/body — two cards on one key could overwrite each other). Settings → Push → weekly section points here (`userpush-techerror-hint`). Push log trigger lists + `userpush.trigger.TECH_ERROR_*`.
      - **Rollback / rolling deploy:** an old pod sends none of these pushes (only the period reminder push) and 404s `/settings/push`; the keys stay in the DB.
      - **Gates (tests):** `TechErrorPushServiceTest` (review 2026-10-18: `approvedOutcomePhrases|textsFitTheConfiguredLimit|decisionUsesTheLiveLimit|sendFailedToSeveral|approvalReminderReportsWhoWasQueued`; 2026-10-19: `queuedDeliveryTellsTheRequesterDirectly|requesterDeliveredTextFitsTheLimit|outcomeTextsFitTheMinimumLimit`), `UserPushServiceTest.enqueueDirect_skipReasonIsARow` (`queued_usernames`), `TechErrorReportSenderTest.pushOutcomes|pushSupersededQueueOwner`, `TechErrorApprovalServiceTest.pushWiring`, `TechErrorReportServiceTest.queuedResultPushesOnce|queuedExecutiveFailureAlsoTellsTheRequester`, `TechErrorReminderServiceTest.approvalReminderPushCountsAsDelivered|approvalReminderDecidesPerPo|approvalReminderSkippedIsFinalPerPo|periodReminderRetriesAreCapped|roundStateFormat|retryPushIsNotBlockedByTheOutboxDedupe|skippedPoIsFinalForTheRound|errorGiveUpIsFailedAndSeparate|periodRepeatZeroStopsForGoodAfterThreeFailures`, `pushTypeLabel.test.jsx`, `TechErrorSettingsControllerTest.getPush|putPush|pushFieldsCoverEveryKey`, `TechErrorReportSettingsTest.pushKeys`, `SettingsScopedAdminGateTest.techErrorPush_globalOnly`, `techErrorPushModel.test.js`, `TechErrorPushSettings.test.jsx`, `UserPushSettings.test.jsx`, `e2e/settings-techerror.spec.js` ("push bildirimleri", 390/768/1280).
    - **Template import, year archive, settings export, UI-language default (2026-10-17, R20, frontend only, user-approved D16 / C12 / E22 / D20; review same day):**
      - **Import:** "Import from another channel" (`te-tpl-import`, `TemplateImport.jsx`, editors only, disabled while saving) lists readable channels from `overview` except this one; inactive ones are labelled "(pasif)". It loads the source template as an unsaved draft through the same path as the versions dialog's "load into the editor" (`loadIntoDraft`): the base version stays this channel's (a concurrent save still 409s), and a dirty draft asks first. When the chosen template's language differs from the draft's (`draftLang`), an inline warning says the report language and month names will change. A read without `sections` loads nothing. A 404 read removes the channel from the list and says so. The source is read with `GET /channels/{id}/template?strict=1` (review 2026-10-18): a corrupt / section-less source template is 409 `TEMPLATE_UNREADABLE` (TR / EN, names the channel) shown in the dialog (`te-tpl-import-unreadable`) and nothing is loaded — it never falls back to the system TR template; the channel's OWN template screen reads without the parameter (lenient, so the team can still repair a corrupt template); an old pod ignores the parameter.
      - **New channel:** the template source defaults to the UI language (`en` → English default, else `tr`).
      - **Year archive:** "Year archive (ZIP)" (`te-year-zip`, `YearArchiveMenu.jsx` + pure `yearArchive.js`) takes ALL reports of the year (not the list filters), in month order, one request at a time via `api.techErrors.pdfBlob` (a network failure / non-JSON error answer comes back as translated text with an `errorInfo`, never the browser's "Failed to fetch"). While the archive runs "Excel'e aktar" is disabled (`data-blocked`, the hint says why — `te.xlsx.waitArchive`) and while the Excel file is prepared the archive menu items are disabled (`te.zip.waitXlsx`): two automatic downloads after one gesture can be blocked by Chrome (review 2026-10-18). 429 honours the server's `Retry-After` (`pdfBlob` returns `retryAfter` seconds from the header or `retry_after_seconds`; `retryWaitMs`: ≤ `ARCHIVE_MAX_RETRY_WAIT_MS` 60 s per wait, 3 s without one), at most 3 retries, all waits of a run ≤ `ARCHIVE_TOTAL_WAIT_BUDGET_MS` 5 min (a wait that would exceed it is skipped — review round 3: the fixed 3 s × 3 lost a second same-minute archive's last months to the per-user 20 / min budget); a month that still gets 429 `RATE_LIMITED` is `limit` in `missing.txt` ("PDF sınırı (dakikalık ya da eşzamanlı)", `te.zip.reason.limit` — the server refuses both its per-minute budget and its one-PDF-at-a-time rule with that code; review round 4), another 429 stays `busy`; while waiting the status line names the real wait (`te.zip.waiting` "{n} sn bekleniyor", `onProgress` `waitMs` — it said "a few seconds" for waits of up to 60 s). Each request has a 5-minute limit combined with the user's cancel (`requestSignal`, `AbortSignal.any` or a linked controller, raced so a hung request never blocks); a timeout makes that month missing ("zaman aşımı"). The first 401 / 403 stops the run: remaining months are listed as not tried, and a 401 fires one `api.sessionPing()` so `request()`'s session path runs.
      - **Streaming ZIP:** written with fflate `Zip` + `ZipPassThrough` (stored). Each PDF is added on arrival through `onFile` and output chunks become Blobs at once — never collect PDFs and `zipSync` them. Names come from `entryNamer` (server name or `<channel>-<year>-<mm>.pdf`, deduplicated, `missing.txt` reserved). Missing months go into `missing.txt` (BOM, CRLF), and cancel downloads nothing.
      - **Leaving:** leaving the channel view while an archive runs (back, open a report) asks first ("Arşivi iptal et ve çık").
      - **Settings export:** Settings → Teknik hata raporu "Export" (`tes-export`, CSV / Excel) writes the filtered, sorted full list (all pages); unknown values write "Bilinmiyor"; counts are numeric in xlsx; `xlsxWriter` is imported on click (`utils/csvExport.downloadBlob`). The approval column follows the screen badge: a channel with no approval flow (`executive_count` 0) writes "—", never "Onaya gönderilmedi".
      - **Errors:** non-API errors in these flows (stale bundle, RangeError, ZIP / Blob / xlsx) go through `utils/errorMessages.clientFailure`: translated text only (stale bundle → `te.pageUpdated`), browser text in `details`.
      - **Gates:** `yearArchive.test.js`, `TechErrorYearArchive.test.jsx` (incl. the archive ↔ Excel lock), `techErrorPdfClient.test.js` (download in the DOM, 60 s URL, translated network text), `TechErrorTemplateImport.test.jsx`, `TechErrorChannelDialogLang.test.jsx`, `techErrorSettingsModel.test.js`, `TechErrorSettingsExport.test.jsx`, `errorMessages.test.js` (clientFailure), `e2e/channel-reports.spec.js` ("başka kanaldan şablon al", "yıl arşivi"), `e2e/settings-techerror.spec.js` ("dışa aktar").
    - **Extra CC + per-channel send log (2026-10-18, R18, user-approved C13 / E22; review same day):**
      - **Extra CC:** `POST /reports/{id}/send` takes an optional `{extra_cc}` (string or array) for THIS send only — `TechErrorReportSender.extraCc`: deliverable addresses (`isDeliverable`), split on `,` `;` whitespace, case-insensitive dedupe, To / existing Cc never re-added, ≤ `MAX_EXTRA_CC` 10 distinct, existing Cc + extra ≤ 20, input ≤ 4000 chars checked before parsing; any breach → 400 `VALIDATION_FAILED` field `extra_cc` naming the address / counts, validated BEFORE the claim (nothing written, not audited). Team delivery: Cc = extras; executive: appended after the team Cc. Never stored on the channel — only in the mail, the `notification_logs.cc` row and the SEND event (`extra_cc`, passive accounts dropped); `Result.cc` / response `cc`; audit counts only. Extra CC never makes a report sent ALONE: with no active To and no active base Cc the extras are not added and the send skips as before (SKIPPED pasif → 409 `MAIL_DISABLED`). The approval auto-send (`decide`) and the test mail never carry extra Cc. No `extra_cc` → the old 4/5-arg calls (byte-identical).
      - **Send dialog:** `SendDialog.jsx` (`te-send-dialog`, field `te-send-extra-cc`) replaces the send `showConfirm` (same title / body); Ctrl/⌘+Enter submits, plain Enter is a new line (the send is irreversible and the field takes one address per line — review round 2; the hint `te.send.ccHint` says so), nothing during IME composition; client mirror `sendModel.parseExtraCc` (format + 10; the 20 total is server-only, its field error shows under the field, dialog stays open). A send response without `cc` after typed extras (old pod) shows `te.sendCcNotConfirmed`. The success toast never prints an empty To list: To empty + Cc → `te.sendDoneCcOnly` (lists the Cc), both empty → `te.sendDonePlain`. Retryable failures keep the dialog OPEN with the typed extra CC (review 2026-10-18 — it used to close, and the next "Send" could go without the extras, irreversibly): `sendModel.sendFailureKind` → `retry` (429 PDF queue / per-user limit — nothing was claimed), `queued` (409 `SEND_QUEUED`, multi-pod Sürüm 2a — locked band + poll of the existing queue, see "Sending") or `unsure` (network / status 0, 409 `SEND_IN_PROGRESS` — a send is running, often the request whose answer was lost —, 503 `DB_UNAVAILABLE` / gateway 502 / 503 / 504 pages and, since review round 4, EVERY other 5xx except 502 `MAIL_FAILED` — a 500 after the hand-off may have sent; review round 3: they used to be `retry`); the error is shown INSIDE the dialog (`te-send-failure`, `data-kind`), focus returns to the field, the report reloads behind it. **Unsure = locked (review round 3 — a second click re-sent the report to the executives because `claimSend` allows re-sending a SENT report):** "Gönder" and Ctrl+Enter stay disabled (`data-locked`) while `ReportEditor.checkUnsureSend` re-reads the report and AWAITS it (`load()` now returns the read data; `busy` 'sendcheck' holds the editor's send / navigation buttons and autosave; Cancel still works); `sendModel.unsureSendOutcome(before, after)` compares with the row from before the click: `sending` / `queued` (`send_state` QUEUED) / `sent` (`last_sent_at` changed or `send_count` grew; review round 4: when the row was already QUEUED before the click, an unchanged old queue or the GET's `reconcileQueued` confirming it is NOT this send — only a moved `last_attempt_at` (stamped by `finishSent` / `finishQueued` / `finishNotSent`, never by `confirmQueued` / `releaseClaim`) counts, and then `send_state` decides; otherwise `notSent`) → the dialog closes with a toast (`te.send.checkedSending|checkedSent`, `te.sendQueued`); `notSent` → a `retry` banner (`te.send.checkedNotSent*`), the typed CC kept, "Gönder" enabled; unreadable → still locked with "Durumu yeniden denetle" (`te-send-recheck`), and closing + reopening keeps the lock, re-checks at once AND opens with the typed extra CC (`ccCarry` → `SendDialog initialCc`, read only on open — review round 4: the banner said the CC was kept but the field opened empty and "Gönder" sent without it; review round 5: only the FIRST reopen after an unsure / not-sent outcome is seeded with that attempt's CC, after that every edit is carried (`SendDialog onEdit(value)`), so a reopen shows what the user last typed, not the pre-failure CC; an emptied field reopens empty, a confirmed send clears it, and the band says "CC kept" only when there is one — `te.send.checkedNotSentNoCc|checkedNotSentClosedNoCc`); a `notSent` that lands while the dialog is closed toasts `te.send.checkedNotSentClosed` and the next open restores that banner + CC (`resumeSend`, only while the report shows no send since that read — `sameSendMark`; cleared by the next attempt; 20.157.0 review: the open RE-READS the report first — `readSendStatus`, busy `sendcheck`, send fields only via `withSendStatus` — and shows the band only when the FRESH row still matches the mark: another tab / person may have sent meanwhile and the band would invite a second send; an unreadable row opens a plain dialog, never "not sent", and the next open re-checks). After `sending` / `queued` (and a normal QUEUED answer) the editor polls the report (`useVisibleInterval`, `SEND_POLL_MS` 10 s, at most `SEND_POLL_MAX_MS` 10 min, sequence-guarded, merges only `SEND_STATUS_FIELDS` via `withSendStatus` — never the draft, version or template) until `sendModel.sendPollStep` is final. Review round 5: the decision is made against the PRE-SEND row (`unsureSendOutcome(before, fresh)` — a released claim (`releaseClaim`: PDF busy, stale approval) puts the row back to its previous state, and reading only the current `send_state` had announced "sent" for a report sent before, the OLD queue's outcome as this send's, and "not settled in 10 minutes" for a never-sent report) or, once THIS attempt's queue is seen (a normal QUEUED answer: the row read right after it, `pollBaselineAfterQueued`; an unsure check or a tick reading the new queue), against that queue (`own`: same `last_attempt_at` → the queue's own result — `confirmQueued` sent, `failQueued` not sent, still queued pending; a later attempt that didn't go out → `superseded`, never "send again" — unless the row shows a DELIVERY since that queue (`send_count` grew or `last_sent_at` moved: the queue was confirmed, then another attempt failed) → `sent`, 20.157.0 review); a claim that is no longer fresh (`send_state` SENDING, status not `sending`) is `unknown`. Toasts `te.send.pollSent|pollNotSent|pollNotSentCc|pollSuperseded` (or `pollUnknown` when the time runs out); "not sent" enables "Gönder" and the next open restores the band + that attempt's CC (`resumeSend`). A poll answer never writes `sent` over a local `changed` set after the request was sent (save answer or image upload / delete — `statusGen`, `withSendStatus(…, { keepChanged })`; a save that STARTED after the request drops the whole answer — `seq`). Only a save answer with `changed: true` bumps `statusGen` (20.157.0 review: a no-op save counted as a local change and kept a stale "changed" badge after the poll read "sent"). **Known limit:** the poll baseline is the editor's LOCAL row from before the click (`before` / the row read right after a QUEUED answer) — the server answer carries no attempt owner, so a send by ANOTHER editor or tab between that row and the poll's read is reported as this user's outcome ("sent" toast, or its queue tracked); the channel's send log names who sent what. Gates `techErrorSendModel.test.js` (sendPollStep rows "gönderim sayısı arttı"), `TechErrorSendDialog.test.jsx` ("DEĞİŞTİRMEYEN kayıt", "bandından ÖNCE rapor yeniden okunur"). A new attempt stops it. Only success and final errors (mail off, no address, approval required, 502 `MAIL_FAILED`…) close it.
      - **Send log data:** `notification_logs.tech_error_channel_id` / `tech_error_report_id` (patch lines + partial index `idx_nl_te_channel (tech_error_channel_id, id) WHERE tech_error_channel_id IS NOT NULL` in the deferred catalog, plain on H2). Every report-bound tech-error `saveLog` calls `TechErrorSendLogService.link` — new trigger paths must too: send, test mail, approval request / decision mails, approver info (`APPROVER_INFO`) and the approval reminder (`TechErrorReminderService.saveLogFor`); the team-level PERIOD reminder covers several channels and stays unlinked (SMTP log only). Older rows are unlinked and not listed. The log row's To and Cc follow ONE passive rule (`TechErrorReportSender.loggedTo` / `loggedCc`): mail service not reached → the requested lists; reached → passive accounts dropped; every To passive but an active Cc → To logged EMPTY (the row stays SENT — the Cc received it; review round 2); everything passive (send skipped) → the requested lists ("why it didn't go").
      - **Send log API:** `GET /channels/{id}/send-log?page&size≤100&kind=all|report|test|approval|reminder&status=all|sent|queued|skipped|failed` (channel read scope, other team 404, 3 queries; `page` clamped to the last page, `clampPage`, the response carries the real page). Rows never carry the raw `email_status` (mail-server text: host, port): `status` class + a fixed `reason` code (`TechErrorSendLogService.REASON_CODES`: QUEUED, MAIL_OFF, INACTIVE_RECIPIENTS, NO_RECIPIENT, NO_APPROVER_EMAIL, TEAM_INACTIVE, MODULE_HIDDEN, APPROVAL_FLOW_REMOVED, SYSTEM_MAINTENANCE, PDF_BUSY, PDF_TOO_LARGE, APPROVAL_STALE, SMTP_FAILED, INTERNAL_ERROR, RETRIES_EXHAUSTED (reminder round gave up after 3 tries, mail server refused — `ST_GAVE_UP`), RETRIES_FAILED (gave up, last try an application error — `ST_GAVE_UP_ERROR`, FAILED class), RETRIED_AFTER_INTERRUPT (a reminder claim whose pod died was retaken — `ST_RETAKEN`, multi-pod Sürüm 2a), SKIPPED_OTHER, UNKNOWN; SENT has none; UI `te.slog.reason.*`, the JS list is read from Java). Reminder statuses are mapped with the reminder service's OWN constants BEFORE the generic rules (review round 2 — "pasif takım" fell into the generic "pasif" = inactive recipients): `ST_PASSIVE` → TEAM_INACTIVE, `ST_HIDDEN` → MODULE_HIDDEN, `ST_NOFLOW` → APPROVAL_FLOW_REMOVED, `ST_MAINTENANCE` (= `SystemMaintenanceService.STATUS_SKIPPED`) → SYSTEM_MAINTENANCE, `ST_NO_APPROVER_EMAIL` → NO_APPROVER_EMAIL (review round 3: the fix is the PO users' AD e-mail, not a team / channel address), `ST_GAVE_UP` → RETRIES_EXHAUSTED, `ST_GAVE_UP_ERROR` → RETRIES_FAILED. A `QUEUED_RETRY…` row older than `TechErrorReportSender.QUEUED_RESULT_WINDOW` (20 min, by `sent_at`) is UNKNOWN ("result never arrived — if the report still shows as queued, send it again"), not QUEUED; its status class (and the `queued` filter) stays queued. `recipient_email` / `cc` are fitted to 255 characters, so `splitFitted` strips the ` … +N` suffix: `to` / `cc` are whole addresses, `to_more` / `cc_more` the overflow — the UI says "+N more", never "full list". Grant-only readers get `to` / `cc` null + `to_count` / `cc_count`; the history endpoint likewise drops `recipients` / `extra_cc` for them (`maskHistoryPeople` → `recipient_count` / `extra_cc_count`).
      - **Send log UI:** `ChannelView` tab `te_tab=sendlog` → `SendLogView.jsx` (`useServerPagination` panel sizes ≤ 100, URL `te_slp` / `te_slps` / `te_slk` / `te_sls`, cleared when leaving the tab; table ≥ 760 px container else cards; recipients wrap; a kind / status / channel change clears the old rows and error at once, page change / Refresh keep them; failed read = danger block + retry, never "no rows"; the refresh-error strip's retry and "clear filters" are ≥ 40 px on touch — `te-sendlog-refresh-retry`, `te-sendlog-clear-filters`).
      - **Rollback:** a pre-R18 pod ignores `extra_cc` (sends without it, no error), writes unlinked log rows and 404s the send log (UI "couldn't be read").
      - **Gates:** `TechErrorReportSenderTest.extraCcRules|extraCcSend|passiveCcNotLogged|passiveToNotLoggedWhenCcReceived|extraCcAloneNeverSends`, `TechErrorReportControllerTest.sendWithExtraCc|sendLog|historyMasksPeopleForGrantOnly`, `TechErrorSendLogServiceTest` (H2: `reasonCodes`, `reminderReasonsMapToTheirOwnCodes`, `queuedOlderThanResultWindowIsUnknown`, `splitFittedMirrorsFitList`, `pageIsClampedToLastPage`, `rowCarriesReasonCodeAndSplitsFittedLists`), `TechErrorApprovalServiceTest` (links, no extra Cc on auto-send), `TechErrorReminderServiceTest.approvalReminderOncePerWindow` (approval reminder rows linked), `techErrorSendModel.test.js`, `TechErrorSendDialog.test.jsx` (retryable failures keep the dialog open), `TechErrorSendLog.test.jsx`, `TechErrorSendLog.mobile.test.jsx`, `TechErrorHistory.test.jsx`, `e2e/channel-reports.spec.js` ("R18", 390/768/1280).
    - **Cross-channel analysis (2026-10-18, R22, user-approved C11: "takımın kanallarında aynı kimlikli alanlar 12 ay × kanal tablo ve grafik"; review same day):**
      - **API:** `GET /api/tech-error-reports/analytics?team=<id|all>&months=6|12|24&fields=<≤ 4 ids>&fresh=1` (`TechErrorAnalyticsService`; read scope = the module's — grant VIEW reads, module visibility applies, unreadable team 404, bad months / fields 400 `VALIDATION_FAILED`, length checked before any pattern). Window = the last N COMPLETED months ending with `defaultPeriod()` (Istanbul).
      - **Inactive channels (2026-10-18, user decision: "pasife alınmış kanalları geçmiş aylarıyla birlikte gösterelim"):** every ACTIVE channel of the scope is listed (also without reports); an INACTIVE channel only when it has ≥ 1 report inside the shown window (no empty rows). Channel rows carry an additive `active` (old server without it → treated as active). Order: active channels (team name, sort order, name), then inactive ones in the same order — chart (≤ 8 lines, inactive ones usually land in "N more channels"), table, cards and CSV. Inclusion is decided by ONE lightweight grouped query (`TechErrorReportRepository.newestPeriodOfInactiveChannels`: newest report period per INACTIVE channel of the readable teams over the REQUESTED window — team IN list, no text columns, no per-channel query; skipped when the readable teams have no inactive channel); full rows are read only for the channels that will be listed. Inactive channels NEVER shrink the month window (review 2026-10-18: one old inactive report cut 24 months to 23 and then vanished itself): the months come from the active channels exactly as before and `truncated` means "months cut", nothing else; when listed active + inactive × months exceed `MAX_CELLS`, inactive channels are dropped from the END of their order → `channels_truncated` + `inactive_omitted`; more than `MAX_CELLS` ACTIVE channels alone are cut from the end too → `active_omitted` (UI note `te-an-active-cut`), and the months banner names `active_count` (review round 2: it used to count inactive channels) — when months are cut as well the inactive note does not claim "the months aren't affected" (`te.an.inactiveCutBody*Months`) (UI note `te-an-inactive-cut`, `te.an.inactiveCut*`; the "only the last N months" banner shows only when `truncated` AND fewer months than requested came back — `analyticsModel.monthsCut`). `channel_count` = active (before the channel cap) + inactive with a report in the SHOWN months (before the cut). Reference type per field = majority of the ACTIVE channels' current templates (tie → first active); only when no active channel defines the field (as number / percent), the LISTED inactive channels' templates (same rule); `type_mismatch` may list inactive channels; `available_fields` include fields only listed inactive channels define, `channels` counts listed channels with the reference type. Team picker (`teams`) = readable teams with an active channel OR an inactive channel with a report in the REQUESTED window — from the same lightweight query over every readable team, so it is identical for every selection and never depends on the active truncation. Activate / deactivate goes through `updateChannel` → `analyticsChanged()` (memo cleared). UI: shadcn `Badge` "Pasif" / "Inactive" (`te.inactive`, `data-slot="te-an-inactive"`, `rounded-md overflow-visible whitespace-normal`) next to the name in the legend, the "N more channels" list, the table header and the cards, each carrying `data-inactive="true"`; CSV header + ` (pasif)` / ` (inactive)` (`te.an.csvInactive`). Gates: `TechErrorAnalyticsServiceTest.inactiveWithReportsListedAfterActive|referenceTypePrefersActiveThenListedInactive|inactiveChannelsKeepStatementCount|fullRowsReadOnlyForListedChannels|repositoryNewestPeriodOfInactiveChannels|teamPickerIncludesTeamsWithListedInactiveOnly|teamPickerIdenticalForEverySelection|inactiveNeverShrinksActiveWindow|activeTruncationThenInactiveFill|deactivationClearsTheMemo`, `TechErrorReportControllerTest.analytics_channelActiveFlag`, `TechErrorReportPostgresTest.analyticsNewestPeriodOfInactiveChannels`, `analyticsModel.test.js` (inactive, cut flags), `TechErrorAnalytics.test.jsx` ("pasif kanallar"), `e2e/channel-reports.spec.js` (analysis, inactive channel with a real-length name, 24-month inactive-cut note).
      - **Values:** reference type per field = the MAJORITY across the scope's ACTIVE channels' current templates (tie → first active channel by team name, sort order, name; listed inactive channels only when no active channel defines the field — see above); channels whose CURRENT template has a different type are listed in the field's `type_mismatch` (UI note "this channel's current template uses another field type; only months with the same type are shown" in the legend, table header and cards — review round 2: the old note said "not compared" although such a channel's OLD reports with the reference type DO count). A month's value counts only when that report's OWN copied template has the SAME id AND type; exempt (`skip_if_no` No), no report or no value → null (never 0). Computed rate / change fields are calculated LIVE like the report screen and the PDF (`TechErrorCalc.apply`; previous month = the same channel's previous calendar month, same id + type) — the read window starts one month before the first month, so there is no per-channel query. If none of the requested fields exist in the scope, the defaults are returned with `dropped_fields` / `fields_fallback` and the UI writes the server's choice into the URL without a second request. A queued, never-sent report is `queued` ("gönderim sırada"), not "not sent". Labels resolve placeholders: `{kanal}` only for a single-channel scope, `{ay}` / `{yil}` from the newest report, otherwise dropped.
      - **Cost:** FIXED whatever the channel count: channels + (only when the readable teams have an inactive channel) the one grouped inactive query + the window read = 2 or 3 statements; the window read covers only LISTED channels in 200-row keyset chunks (`id > after ORDER BY id`), only numeric / yes-no values and targets are kept, distinct template texts are parsed once; listed channels × months ≤ `MAX_CELLS` (1200; active channels × months over it → newest months kept + `truncated`) and listed channels ≤ `MAX_CELLS` → rows ≤ 2 × `MAX_CELLS`, ≤ 14 chunks (`MAX_CHUNKS`). Memo = `TtlMemo` 60 s (key = readable team set + params + a generation, failures never stored); `fresh=1` recomputes at most every 5 s. `TechErrorReportService.analyticsChanged` (optional bean, after commit; no transaction → at once) calls `invalidate()` (generation + 1 — a compute that started before keeps writing to the old key — and clear) on a values save that changed something, report create / delete, template refresh, channel update / delete and template save (review round 2).
      - **UI:** `te_view=analytics` (lazy `analytics/TechErrorAnalytics`, opened by `te-analytics-open` on the channel list toolbar; URL `te_an_team` / `te_an_months` / `te_an_fields`, kept within the tab): per field a line chart (≤ 8 channels — non-status tokens, 4 solid + 4 dashed —, the rest under "N more channels"; empty month = gap, lone value = `te-an-dot`; the Y axis fits the data, `yAxisScale` — counts never below 0 unless negatives exist, percents within 0–100, short tick labels fall back to the full format when two would read the same) + months × channels table (container ≥ 760 px, newest month first, sticky month column) else per-channel cards (`@container`, `@xl:grid-cols-2`); cells formatted like the PDF, null "—", unsent month muted + sr "not sent", a cell opens its report (≥ 40 × 40 px on touch) with `te_from=analytics` and the report's back button says "Analize dön" and returns with the filters — that return's first read is `fresh=1` (`TechErrorReportsPage.returnToAnalytics` → `returning`, kept until the first successful answer; opening from the channel list is not fresh); CSV of the visible table (numbers via `csvNumber`, unit in the header, empty month empty). A filter change clears the old data at once (skeleton), Refresh keeps it; late responses are dropped (sequence). The toolbar also shows on a first-load error, with "Varsayılana dön" clearing every `te_an_*` key (`ANALYTICS_KEYS`) — except 403, which shows only the no-access state; a load failure is a danger block + retry (≥ 40 px on touch), never "no data". The response carries `team_name` for a selected team; the picker never renders a bare `#id` (names from the answers, "Takım yükleniyor…" while reading, `te.an.teamUnnamed` when unknown).
      - **Gates:** `TechErrorAnalyticsServiceTest` (H2: same id + type, exempt, live calc with a corrected previous month, majority type, `flaggedChannelOldReportsWithReferenceTypeStillCount`, fallback fields, truncation, fixed 2 statements, chunks, memo, `reportWritesClearTheMemo`), `TechErrorReportControllerTest.analytics_*`, `analyticsModel.test.js` (reads the Java limits; `yAxisScale`, `csvNumber`), `TechErrorAnalytics.test.jsx` (sequence guard, clear on filter change, toolbar on error, fresh on return, 403 without toolbar, team name), `lazyChunks.test.js`, `e2e/channel-reports.spec.js` ("analiz", 390/768/1280, `narrowCells`).
    - **Value export + channel Excel (2026-10-18, R17, user-approved E23 / C14; two review rounds same day):**
      - **API:** `GET /api/tech-error-reports/export?team=<id|all>&channel=<id>&year=&month=&status=sent|all&format=json|csv` (`TechErrorReportExportController`, a separate controller on the same base path; `TechErrorExportService`; read-only `TechErrorReportExportRepository` whose projection selects NO people column). Sign-in is the session only (no API key): the single-active-session rule applies, so a BI job needs its OWN account — pulling with a person's account signs that person out.
      - **Access:** the module read gate (`tech_error_reports.read/view` + module visibility → 403) + `canRead` per team / channel (team access grants VIEW+ included, no 7/24 widening); an unreadable team / channel or a team ≠ the channel's team → 404. `year` 2020…current Istanbul year, required except for a single `channel` (all years); `month` needs `year`; bad values → 400 `VALIDATION_FAILED` + `field` (no quota spent).
      - **Reads:** `reconcileQueued` for the in-scope channels first (never on a 400 / 404 / 429). One row per report × number / percent / yesno / text field of the report's OWN copied template (textarea / images / tables / issues excluded); exempt → value null + `exempt`. Calculated fields are LIVE like `pdfInput` (`TechErrorCalc.applyDetailed`; previous month = the same channel's previous calendar month via `sameTypeFields(Template, Values, Template)`, pulled into the ONE report query with an `EXISTS` so drafts count as inputs); `computed` = status OK. 18 columns (`COLUMNS`), the last `calc_status` = the `TechErrorCalc` status of a field with a formula (OK calculated; MISSING / ZERO / NEGATIVE / EQUAL / RANGE no result, value empty; MANUAL / BAD_REF the typed value is kept; null without a formula or exempt). `report_status` = `TechErrorReportService.reportStatus`; `changed_after_send` = `changedAfterSend` (true while a send claim runs too); `approval_state` only for channels with an approval flow and follows the channel's CURRENT executive list (`notes` say so). Numbers as plain decimals (≤ 6 decimals).
      - **Cost / limits:** a COUNT query first (over `MAX_REPORTS` 5 000 → 400 `EXPORT_TOO_LARGE`, no JSON loaded, no audit), then ONE load (≤ 2 × the count, re-checked); loaded rows are parsed once and their JSON dropped at once (template cache keyed by SHA-256), only field and target values are kept. 6 per user per minute (429 `RATE_LIMITED` + `Retry-After`); ONE export per user at a time (waiting or running, until the response — CSV or JSON — is fully written) — a second request gets 429 `EXPORT_BUSY` at once, quota not spent; ≤ 2 exports prepared per pod — the permit covers only the read and the live calculation and is released BEFORE the response is written; when full, wait ≤ 5 s, then 429 `EXPORT_BUSY` + `Retry-After: 10` (quota refunded); after 12 busy refusals per user per minute → 429 `RATE_LIMITED` without waiting.
      - **Formats:** JSON `{columns, rows (arrays), notes (Msg.t), reports, report_count, row_count, max_reports, filter}` inside the usual `{success, data}`, STREAMED with a Jackson `JsonGenerator` from the app's ObjectMapper (`writeJson`, review 2026-10-18 — byte-identical to the old `ResponseEntity` body, test oracle; `AUTO_CLOSE_CONTENT` off so a write cut halfway never closes into a valid-looking partial body): through the message converter `UserRefResponseAdvice` (`UserRefWire.toOpaqueFast`) turned the whole body of a non-global viewer into ONE String (≈ 100–150 MB at the cap — several pulls could OOM a 1 GiB pod); the stream bypasses the advice and needs none — no column / key is a user ref (gate `jsonCarriesNoUserRefKeys`; a new column must keep it that way); CSV streamed (BOM, `Csv.cell` formula guard for text, server-generated plain decimals written raw so a negative percent stays a number), `attachment; filename="teknik-hata-raporlari-<yıl>[-<ay>].csv"` (`-tum-yillar` for one channel's years), `no-store` — the CSV is for BI tools (comma separated; Excel users take the .xlsx button). Audit `TECH_ERROR_REPORT_EXPORT` (filter + report / row counts).
      - **UI (C14):** `ChannelXlsxButton` (`te-channel-xlsx`) in the channel view's report toolbar: one channel, `status=all`, the selected year (list status / search filters are NOT applied — the hint `te.xlsx.hintYear` says so) → lazy `channelExport.js` (`buildXlsx` + `utils/csvExport.downloadBlob` — anchor in the DOM, URL kept 60 s; review 2026-10-18: the old `triggerDownload` (anchor outside the DOM, URL revoked right after `click()`) did not start the download on Safari / iPad while the success toast showed — "PDF indir" (`api.techErrors.downloadPdf`) likewise; `downloadBlob` returns `true` / `false` and never throws (review round 3): `downloadPdf` returns `{ success: false, code: 'DOWNLOAD_FAILED' }` when the browser could not start the download (toast `te.pdfDownloadFailed`), the year archive toasts `te.zip.downloadFailed` instead of "downloaded"); pure `channelExportModel.js` reads columns by NAME (`pivotExport`: row key = field id + TYPE, label from the newest month, months ascending; `buildSheets`: "Değerler" + "Raporlar", approval column only with a flow; `exportFileName` `<slug>-<year|y1-y2>-degerler.xlsx`, slug cut at a word boundary ≤ 60). A server / network error shows the server text with details; a chunk-load / build failure shows `te.xlsx.buildFailedText` (browser text only under "Teknik ayrıntı").
      - **Rollback:** a pre-R17 pod 404s the export path; the button shows the server's 404 text.
      - **Gates:** `TechErrorExportServiceTest` (H2: own template, exempt, live calc incl. a corrected August and the year boundary, `calc_status`, COUNT cap, statement count), `TechErrorReportExportControllerTest` (scope, validation, quota, per-user busy, permit released before streaming, busy budget, `jsonStreamIsByteIdenticalToTheOldBody`, `jsonCarriesNoUserRefKeys`, `markerHeldWhileJsonIsWritten`), `TechErrorReportServiceTest.exportSeesReconciledQueuedSendAsSent|changedAfterSendWhileSending`, `TechErrorReportPostgresTest.exportQueries`, `CsvExportGuardTest`, `channelExportModel.test.js` (fixtures checked against the Java `COLUMNS`), `TechErrorChannelXlsx.test.jsx`, `lazyChunks.test.js`, `auditFormat.test.js`, `e2e/channel-reports.spec.js` ("Excel'e aktar", 390/768/1280, real download).
    - **Team template library + report template version (2026-10-18, R21, user-approved D17 / D19; review rounds same day):**
      - **Library data:** `tech_error_team_templates` (`TechErrorTeamTemplateService`; ≤ 20 per team, unique Turkish `name_key`, `@Version` + `@DynamicUpdate`, section / field / slot counts and lang as columns — list rows never carry the template JSON). At most ONE default per team: every write locks the team row (`lockTeamRow`, `SELECT … FOR UPDATE`) and clears other defaults with a conditional bulk UPDATE (version +1); PostgreSQL partial unique index `uq_tett_one_default` (patch line, recognized by `SchemaDdlGuard`; a violation → 409 `CONCURRENT_MODIFICATION`). Limit and name uniqueness are checked under the lock; a unique race → 409, never 500.
      - **API:** `/api/tech-error-reports/teams/{teamId}/templates[/{id}]` (`TechErrorTeamTemplateController`): read AND write = the team's OWN scope (`canReadLibrary` = `ownVisibleTeamIds`; writes `canWriteOwn` + `.crud`) — team access grants → 404, AUDIT / a scoped admin with view scope read only (403 on writes), another team's entry → 404, module visibility applies. Strict template validation on write (64 KB, field `template`; name field `name`), reads use `TechErrorTemplate.readStored`, which NEVER falls back to the system template: an unreadable entry → 409 `TEMPLATE_UNREADABLE` on read and on channel create. The same rule for a channel COPY source (review 2026-10-18): `createChannel` reads `copy_from` with `readStored` → 409 `TEMPLATE_UNREADABLE` field `copy_from` (shown under the copy picker; `TechErrorReportController.unreadableChannelTemplate`), never the system TR template. PUT = only the fields sent + `version` (stale → 409 `CONCURRENT_MODIFICATION`); a no-op PUT writes and audits nothing; the update audit's `name_before` is read under the lock (`UpdateResult.nameBefore`). 409 `TEMPLATE_LIBRARY_LIMIT` / `TEMPLATE_NAME_EXISTS`. Audit `TECH_ERROR_TEAM_TEMPLATE_CREATE|UPDATE|DELETE`; retention `tech-error-team-templates-orphan`. Overview team rows carry `library_read`.
      - **Channel create:** takes `library_template_id` (looked up only in the new channel's team; `readStored` → strict re-validation; 400 field `library_template_id` for a missing / foreign entry); the response carries `template_source {kind, library_template_id, name}`; the channel's v1 template version stores `source_kind` library | copy | default + `source_name` (hidden from grant-only readers); the create audit says `source: library` + the entry name.
      - **UI:** `TemplateLibrary.jsx` + pure `templateLibraryModel.js`. New-channel dialog: fourth source card "Takım kütüphanesinden" — the team default is preselected unless the user touched the source (ANY card click / key counts, even on the already-selected TR card); while the library loads and the source is untouched, "Kanalı aç" waits with a note (a FAILED read never blocks — TR / EN stay usable, retry offered; unread ≠ empty); reload on team change (sequence guard); the "default preselected" hint shows only while that automatic choice stands; a response without `template_source` after a library choice (old pod) warns `te.lib.notApplied`. Template editor: "Kütüphaneye kaydet" (new or overwrite, optional team default, field errors under the fields; a 404 in "new" mode — old pod, team deleted meanwhile — is a banner `te.lib.err.newGone`, the target field error only in overwrite mode; the overwrite card says "loading" / "couldn't be read" while the list is loading / unreadable, "none yet" only when read and empty — review 2026-10-18) and "Varsayılana dön" as a menu (system TR / EN + library entries), loaded as an unsaved draft on the CURRENT base version (a concurrent save still 409s); an unreadable entry leaves the draft unchanged; an entry (or system default) in another language than the draft says so in the confirm (`te.lib.resetLangWarn`, like the import's `te.imp.langWarn`). Channel list: `LibraryManagerDialog` (table ≥ 640 px container else cards; rename, set / unset default, delete with a confirm naming the entry); the list stays mounted while the same team reloads ("Liste yenileniyor…", no skeleton) and focus returns to the row's menu trigger after an action, to the next / previous row after a delete, to the close button when the list is empty, to the row after a cancelled delete.
      - **D19 report template version:** `tech_error_reports.template_version` (patch line) set at report creation and on "apply latest template". `TechErrorReportTemplateVersionBackfill` (marker `2026-10-18-tech-error-report-template-version-v2` — never rename; 10 min after boot, own `scheduler_lock`, marker re-read inside the lock, written only after a full pass, ≤ 24 failed attempts per pod, 4-minute budget per run with a per-pod cursor): fills NULLs AND corrects values that differ from the report's newest CREATE / TEMPLATE_REFRESH history event (conditional UPDATE on the value read); no event → "unknown". Versions list `report_count` per version (one grouped query) + `unknown_version_reports` / `unlisted_version_reports` (versions pruned or never recorded); the version detail lists ≤ 24 report periods. UI: "Bu sürümle N rapor" (0 with unknowns → "bilinen rapor yok"), v1 source line, report chip `te-report-tpl-version` ("Şablon v{n}", never v0 / vnull), hint `te-channel-tpl-version` ("Kanal şablonu v{m}") next to "apply latest template".
      - **Rollback / rolling deploy:** an old pod ignores `library_template_id` and creates from the system TR template — the client warns (`te.lib.notApplied`). An old pod's "apply latest template" after the v2 marker was written leaves the report's `template_version` (chip and version counts) on the older number; the report content is correct; the one-time backfill does not fix it later. Avoid creating channels / refreshing templates during the rollout.
      - **Gates:** `TechErrorTeamTemplateServiceTest` (incl. a two-thread set-default race), `TechErrorTeamTemplateControllerTest`, `TechErrorReportTemplateVersionBackfillTest` (nested `Job`: fill, correct stale, conditional write, cursor resume, marker v2), `TechErrorTeamTemplatePostgresTest` (partial index, FOR UPDATE wait, violation → 409), `TechErrorReportPostgresTest` (template-version queries), `TechErrorReportControllerTest.createChannelFromLibrary|overviewLibraryRead|templateVersionsReportCounts|reportCarriesTemplateVersion|unreadableChannelTemplateIsNotReplacedBySystemDefault`, `TechErrorTemplateLibrary(.mobile|Review).test.jsx`, `TechErrorTemplateVersionCounts.test.jsx`, `templateLibraryModel.test.js`, `e2e/channel-reports.spec.js` ("takım şablon kütüphanesi", 390/768/1280).
