    - **E-mail preview (2026-10-17, release 10, user request: "mail göndermeden önce overview edebilmeliyiz"):** `GET /api/tech-error-reports/reports/{id}/mail-preview` (read scope like the PDF; `TechErrorReportSender.preview(report, channel, team, delivery)`: the SAME path as a send — `service.renderPdf` (same ladder, same permit, 429 `PdfBusyException`) with `mailImagePreparer` inside the loader, then the SAME mail cap rule (PDF + inline bytes > `max-mail-mb` → images PDF-only; the PDF alone over the cap → `problem PDF_TOO_LARGE`, body still shown) and `buildFull` with the real file name — NO claim / mail / `notification_logs` / content revision; the response carries `pdf_bytes` + `max_mail_mb`; recipients computed like the send but an empty list is a `problem` code (`TEAM_EMAIL_MISSING` / `EXEC_RECIPIENTS_MISSING`, before `PDF_TOO_LARGE`), never an exception; `cid:` image references become `data:` URIs ONLY in the preview (`inlineCids`, replaced WITH the closing quote — `te-img-tech-ratio` is a prefix of `te-img-tech-ratio-monthly`; the header's `cid:brand-logo` likewise, `inlineBrandLogo`, variant `ok` like the send); the editor runs `ensureSaved()` before opening the preview (a dirty draft would otherwise preview stale content and send new content); per-user limit shared with `GET /reports/{id}/pdf` (review 2026-10-18 — each call renders a full PDF on the pod's 2 permits that sending uses): ONE at a time per user (`rendering`, 429 `RATE_LIMITED` + `Retry-After: 5`, not counted) and `RENDER_PER_MINUTE` 20 per minute (a year archive's 12 PDFs fit; 429 `RATE_LIMITED` + `Retry-After`), checked AFTER the read scope (a 404 spends nothing; a PDF-queue refusal — 429 `PdfBusyException` — is refunded, so the year archive's retries don't eat the budget), never on send / approval / test mail; the dialog never trips the one-at-a-time rule itself (review round 3 — in StrictMode the second effect's request got 429 and the first answer was dropped, so `npm run dev` always showed "couldn't be read"): every read has its own `AbortController` (aborted in the effect cleanup, result ignored) and goes through `techerror/mailPreviewFetch.fetchMailPreview`, which JOINS an in-flight request for the same user × report × report `version` × image revision × request language (X-Lang) instead of sending a second one (review round 4: close → edit → reopen ran `ensureSaved` and then joined a request started before the save — stale preview, new content sent; the dialog gets `reportVersion`; review round 5: an image upload / delete doesn't change the version, so the editor calls `bumpMailPreviewContent(reportId)` on every image change — a module-level counter per report, the key takes the larger of it and the dialog's `contentRev` prop, which also reloads an open dialog); an entry for another version / revision nobody waits for is aborted at once, and a new request that gets the one-at-a-time 429 (`RATE_LIMITED` + `Retry-After`) waits the suggested time and is retried up to `MAIL_PREVIEW_BUSY_RETRIES` (2) times while the waits stay within `MAIL_PREVIEW_BUSY_RETRY_TOTAL_S` (15 s) in total (`busyRetryWaitMs(res, budget)`; review round 5: one 5 s retry failed when the previous render took longer), then the server's message is shown (the per-minute 429's long wait is not retried); the network request is aborted only `MAIL_PREVIEW_KEEP_MS` (5 s) after the last caller left (a quick reopen joins it), finished answers are not cached, the key carries `storageOwner()` (another user never joins); the dialog body scrolls (`te-mail-body`) between a fixed header and footer, so 20 To + 20 Cc + three banners on a 390 px phone never push the buttons out of view; delivery = `approvalRequired(ch) ? EXECUTIVE : TEAM`; a grant-only reader gets `to`/`cc` empty + `to_count`/`cc_count` + `recipients_hidden` (people rule). UI: `ReportEditor` "E-postayı önizle" (`te-mail-preview-btn`, next to PDF, for every reader) → `MailPreviewDialog` (subject / To + delivery badge / Cc / attachment + image counts, problem banner, desktop 1040 px ↔ phone 400 px `SegmentedControl`, `iframe sandbox="" srcDoc` `te-mail-frame` `data-width`, "Gönder" only when the editor's own send would be enabled (`onSend` → closes and runs the existing confirm + send), `te.mail.*` keys). **Provenance (same day, user request: "oluşturan kişi, onaylayan kişi bilgileri gözükmeli"):** `TechErrorPdfInput.Provenance` (created by / at, updated by / at, `approvalState` null = no approval flow else none | pending | approved | rejected | stale, approved by / at ONLY for `approved` = APPROVED ∧ `approval_rev` = COALESCE(`content_rev`, 0)) filled by `TechErrorReportService.provenance(r, ch)` inside `pdfInput`; `TechErrorReportMail.provenanceLines` is the single text source for the mail band (`te-mail-provenance` lines: "Oluşturan: A · dd.MM.yyyy HH:mm", "Son düzenleyen: B · …" only when different, "Onaylayan: C · …" or "Onay: <state>", then "Üretim: …") AND the PDF cover (band grows 10 pt per extra line); the old 11-arg constructor (provenance null) keeps the old single "Hazırlayan · Üretim" line byte-identically. The preview response carries `provenance {created_by, created_at, updated_by, updated_at, approval_state, approved_by, approved_at}` for EVERY reader (document provenance, written in the body too — not a people list); the dialog shows the rows (`te-mail-created|updated|approved|approval`), and the report header shows "Oluşturan <date> · <name>" (`te-created`, `te.createdLine`). Gates: `TechErrorReportMailTest.provenanceLines`, `TechErrorReportPdfWriterTest.coverProvenanceLines`, `TechErrorReportServiceTest.provenanceFromRow`. **Review round (same day):** the footer's "why you received this" follows the audience — `TechErrorReportMail.Audience` TEAM (old text, byte-identical) / EXECUTIVE (channel's executive recipient, team in Cc, "+ approved by PO X on …" when the provenance is approved) / APPROVER (approval request mail) via the 6-arg `buildFull`; `TechErrorApprovalService.approvers` really nulls the e-mail of a second PO sharing a mailbox (one request mail, one token; both still approve in-app); the approval link page's `lang` + description follow the request language. Gates: `TechErrorReportControllerTest.mailPreview|pdfAndPreviewPerUserRate|pdfAndPreviewOnePerUserAtATime`, `TechErrorReportSenderTest.previewBuildsWithoutSending` (PDF_TOO_LARGE, sum cap, cid prefix), `TechErrorReportMailTest.footerWhyByAudience`, `TechErrorApprovalServiceTest.approversSharedMailboxDedupe`, `TechErrorMailPreview.test.jsx` (incl. StrictMode double effect), `techErrorMailPreviewFetch.test.js`, `e2e/channel-reports.spec.js` ("e-posta önizlemesi" incl. the 20 To + 20 Cc + three banners case, 390/768/1280).
    - **Test mail to self + recipient summary + review fixes (2026-10-17, user requests "bana test gönder" and the module review):** `POST /api/tech-error-reports/reports/{id}/mail-preview/test` (same read gate as the preview — a grant-only reader may use it, the mail goes only to themselves): the SESSION user needs a deliverable address (409 `NO_EMAIL`, checked before the quota), 3 per minute per user per pod (429 `RATE_LIMITED` + `Retry-After`); an attempt that never reached the mail service (PDF queue full → 429, `PDF_TOO_LARGE` → 409) gives the reservation back (`releaseTestMail`), SENT / QUEUED / SKIPPED / FAILED spend it. `TechErrorReportSender.sendTest` renders ONCE through the same private `render()` as `preview()` (same PDF ladder / permit / images / file name / footer audience of the real delivery), subject `[TEST] ` + title, a warning block after the band in the tester's UI language (`testIntro`), PDF attached; NO `claimSend`, `touchContent`, history event, `last_sent_*` or approval check. ONE `notification_logs` row per attempt (trigger `TECH_ERROR_REPORT_TEST`, role `SELF`); the audit `TECH_ERROR_REPORT_TEST_MAIL` has no address and its `reason` is the error CODE (`failureReason`: `PDF_BUSY`, `PDF_TOO_LARGE`, … — also used by `/send`). The preview returns `pdf_too_large` as a separate flag next to `problem` (which names the recipient issue first); `MailPreviewDialog` hides "Bana test gönder" (`te-mail-test`) only on it (old server: `pdf_bytes > max_mail_mb`) and can show the recipient strip and the PDF strip together. **Approval link = in-app gates** (`TechErrorApprovalService.linkDecisionAllowed`): `tech_error_reports.approve/execute` + `tech_error_reports.read/view` (`PermissionService.allows(systemRole, …)`, no session) + the module enabled for the report's team (`TechErrorVisibilityService`: no bean = unrestricted, unreadable = closed); otherwise reason `no_permission` — the page says why, no form, the token is NOT consumed, `TECH_ERROR_APPROVAL_LINK_DENIED reason=no_permission`. Deliberate difference: the link does not exempt a global viewer. **Superseded links:** `request()` no longer deletes the previous request's tokens — `tech_error_approval_tokens.superseded_at` (`patch()` line; NULL = live) is stamped (`supersedeByReportId`), superseded + expired rows of the report are pruned on the next request (`deleteSupersededExpired`); `status()` order used → superseded → expired → not_found → not_pending → approver_inactive → no_permission; `markUsed` stamps only a token that is unused AND not superseded (0 rows → re-read → used / superseded / not_found, no decision); the superseded page also points to deciding in Site Monitor. **Recipient caps never drop silently:** `parseAddressesDetailed` = `{addresses, dropped}` (`MAX_RECIPIENTS` 20; `parseAddresses` / `recipients` byte-identical, Admin → Teams validation unchanged); To overflow → `to_dropped` / history `dropped_recipients`; on executive delivery the Cc is the team list MINUS the executives, then capped at 20 (`ccAddresses`; before the fix it was capped first) → `cc_dropped`, WARN, history `dropped_cc`, strips `te-mail-dropped` / `te-mail-cc-dropped`, history notes `te-history-send-dropped` / `te-history-send-cc-dropped`. **Recipient summary:** `GET /reports/{id}` adds `team_email_count` (≤ 20) + `team_email_dropped`; `RecipientsSummary` (`te-recipients`) under the report title: "Takım adresi: N" (0 → "yok", destructive; dropped → "20 (+N gitmez)", warning), with an approval flow "Yönetici alıcı: M" + the approval-state chip (`ApprovalCard.approvalStateLabelKey` / `APPROVAL_TONE`); a MISSING count draws no chip (unknown ≠ 0). **Phone action bar:** `hooks/useElementHeight` (ResizeObserver, border-box) sets the report editor's bottom padding = bar height + 16 px (`pb-24` only while unmeasured); "E-postayı önizle" stays a button at every width. Gates: `TechErrorReportSenderTest.sendTest_*|recipients|droppedRecipientsAreCountedAndRecorded`, `TechErrorReportControllerTest.mailPreviewTest|mailPreviewTest_failedAttemptsRefundQuota|reportDetailRecipientCounts|mailPreview`, `TechErrorApprovalServiceTest.approvePermissionGate|resendSupersedesOldLinks|supersededBetweenStatusAndMarkUsed|linkNeedsReadPermissionAndModuleVisibility`, `TechErrorApprovalLinkControllerTest.deniedReasons` (own IP per case — the 30/min limiter is shared across the class), `TechErrorReportServiceTest.sendEventCarriesDroppedRecipients`, `useElementHeight.test.js`, `TechErrorMailPreview.test.jsx`, `TechErrorRecipientsSummary.test.jsx`, `TechErrorHistory.test.jsx`, `e2e/channel-reports.spec.js` ("e-posta önizlemesi" test-mail step; "rapor:" last section ends above the action bar).

  - **Anomaly topics (2026-10-18, user request: "konu başlığı; NE ZAMAN — gün + saat aralığı; kaç teknik hata ve tüm teknik hatalar içindeki payı otomatik; kesinti kaynaklı mı — Evet ise Kesinti ID zorunlu; müşteri etkisi var mı — yoksa müşteri etkisi gösterilmesin; isteğe bağlı görseller; rapor metinleri bunlara göre üretilsin"):**
    - **Template (`TechErrorTemplate.IssueOptions`, 16th `Section` component `options`, `opts()` never null):** per `issues` section `ask_time` + `time_label`, `ask_outage` + `outage_label` + `outage_id_label`, `ask_impact` + `impact_label` + `no_impact_display` (`hide_count` = customers not asked / printed, the topic says "Müşteri etkisi yok" | `hide_topic` = the topic is left out of the PDF and the mail; `NO_IMPACT_DISPLAY`), `allow_images` (≤ `MAX_ISSUE_IMAGES` 2 per topic, full width, optional caption), `ratio_of` (id of the "all technical errors" field). A section whose questions were NEVER configured (`options == null`, no key in the JSON) writes no key (an older template is byte-identical — no false "template changed"); a configured section — any of the four switch keys or `ratio_of` present, even all off (review 2026-10-18) — writes the four booleans (`false` when off) and only the labels of switches that are on, so the migration can tell "the team turned everything off" from "never configured"; strict save rejects a non-boolean switch, an unknown display or a malformed `ratio_of` id, a missing / non-number `ratio_of` is ACCEPTED (the editor warns — removing a field must not lock the template, same rule as summary / trend); a blank label becomes today's default in the TEMPLATE language; lenient read drops bad values. No switch on (`legacy()`, `options == null`) = the pre-2026-10-18 layout everywhere (customers always asked, PDF / mail print the old counts line byte-identically). Default TR / EN template: all on, `ratio_of = error-count` (`IssueOptions.defaults`).
    - **Values (`TechErrorValues.Issue`, written only when set — old JSON byte-identical):** `date` (`yyyy-MM-dd`, a REAL day, strict: inside the report's month — `normalize(raw, t, YearMonth)`, the message names the month; lenient read keeps an out-of-month date), `start` / `end` (`HH:mm`, both or none, a range needs a date; end after start, or `end_next_day` = past midnight with end ≤ start, at most 24 h — decided: explicit "Ertesi gün bitti" checkbox, never an implicit wrap), `outage` (yes/no) + `outage_id` (required on Yes, trimmed, ≤ 60; dropped on No), `impact` (customers kept only on Yes; an unanswered impact with customers entered is inferred — > 0 Yes, 0 No — for reports from before the question / an older pod), `captions {"1","2"}` (≤ 200), `share` (server-computed, client value ignored). Errors are keyed `issues.<sid>.<issue id>.<field>` (all issue fields now, incl. topic / title / description / customers / errors — the index key `issues.<sid>.<n>` is gone; the client still shows it from an old pod). `TechErrorValues.conform` fits topics to a section's questions (lenient rules) — `carry` uses it.
    - **Share (`TechErrorIssueShare`, applied inside `TechErrorCalc.applyDetailed` → save, template refresh, `pdfInput` LIVE, export / sample):** `errors ÷ fields[ratio_of] × 100`, `TechErrorCalc.roundRate` (2 decimals HALF_UP, small values 2 significant digits); statuses OK | MISSING (`missing` errors / total) | ZERO | OVER (topic count > total — no share) | EXEMPT (total answered No) | BAD_REF; never a fake 0. Truth table `TechErrorIssueShareTest.SHARE_TABLE`, read by `techErrorIssueShare.test.js` (JS `issueShare`, same rounding `roundCalc`). Not in history diffs (derived).
    - **Lead text (`TechErrorIssueText.lead`, JS `issueLeadText`, truth table `TechErrorIssueTextTest.LEAD_TABLE`):** one paragraph in the TEMPLATE language — TR "12 Eylül 2026, 14:00–15:30 arasında **1.250** teknik hata alındı; bu, dönemdeki tüm teknik hataların **%3,4** kadarıdır. Kesinti kaynaklıdır (Kesinti ID: **INC-2026-0915**). Müşteri etkisi: **320** müşteri." (overnight "23:30 – ertesi gün 01:15", date only "… tarihinde", no time "Bu konu nedeniyle …", no errors "… yaşandı.", "Kesinti kaynaklı değildir.", "Müşteri etkisi var." / "Müşteri etkisi yok."; impact not asked → "<customers_label>: **n**."); EN "On 12 September 2026, 14:00–15:30, **1,250** technical errors occurred; this is **3.4%** of …". Unasked / unanswered parts drop (no half sentence), numbers and the ID bold (an ID with `*` stays plain), labels resolved + neutralised. The description follows it. Same text on screen (card preview), PDF and mail; no Turkish suffix after a number ("%3,4 kadarıdır"). Counts keep their abbreviation (rebase onto the 2026-10-18 number display): a topic error / customer count typed "1,25M" (unit key `issues.<sid>.<issue id>.errors|customers`) prints "1,25M" in the lead (`lead(s, i, share, lang, resolve, errorsUnit, customersUnit)`, JS `issueLeadText(…, errorsUnit, customersUnit)` through `formatCount` = `TechErrorFormat.count(n, lang, unit)`; the 5-argument form is byte-identical), in the card chips, the legacy counts line, the PDF and the mail; the share is computed from the NUMBER (1.250.000 — units never affect math; `TechErrorIssueShare.apply` keeps `units` when it rebuilds the values, it used to drop them); no zero tick on topic counts (number display decision). The card gets only its OWN two exponents (`errorsUnit` / `customersUnit` primitives — the memo), `onUnit` = the editor's stable `setUnit`. LEAD_TABLE rows carry `eu=` / `cu=`; gates `TechErrorIssueTextTest`, `TechErrorIssueShareTest.shareUsesNumbersAndKeepsUnits`, `TechErrorReportPdfWriterTest.anomalyAbbreviatedCounts` (PDF + mail + legacy line, no tick), `TechErrorAnomalyIssues.test.jsx` (kısaltma), `e2e/channel-reports.spec.js` ("anomali konusu": "1,25M" → lead + chip + share %2,5 + saved `errors: 1250000` with unit 6).
    - **Topic images:** slot id `iss:<section>:<issue>:<1|2>` (`issueSlotId` / `parseIssueSlot`), same validator and 40 MB report total; `tech_error_report_images.slot_id` widened to 120 (patch `ALTER COLUMN … TYPE VARCHAR(120)`, entity length 120). Upload only to a topic SAVED in the stored values of a section that allows images (400 field `slot` otherwise) — the client saves first (`ReportEditor.beforeIssueUpload`, after the image passed `prepareImage`). A save that removes a topic (or its section stops allowing images) deletes its images in the same transaction, each an `IMAGE_DELETE` event with the topic's name and `reason: topic_removed` (history note `te.hist.imgTopicRemoved`); the deleted slots ride in `SaveOutcome.removedImages` and the controller ALWAYS audits them (`image_removed` + `images_removed` + `reason: topic_removed`, also when an auto-save merged its history — review 2026-10-18). Upload, values save, template refresh and the orphan sweep LOCK the report row first (`TechErrorReportRepository.lockRow`, `SELECT … FOR UPDATE`, H2 too — review 2026-10-18: an unlocked upload read the stored values at transaction start, a concurrent save deleting the topic could not see the uncommitted image, and the image survived the topic; gate `TechErrorReportServiceTest.uploadWaitsForTopicRemoval`). LOCK ORDER (review 2026-10-18, round 2): EVERY transaction that writes a report's images or its row takes that lock as its FIRST step — also image delete and report delete; channel delete locks all its reports in ascending id order (`lockForChannel`, `ORDER BY id FOR UPDATE`) before deleting anything. Image / report / channel delete used to remove image rows first and touch the report row last (`touchContent` / `deleteIfAllowed`) → PostgreSQL deadlock (40P01) against a replace upload holding the report row. A lock conflict that still happens answers 503 `LOCK_CONFLICT` + `Retry-After: 2` (not 409 — autosave keeps retrying instead of showing the conflict banner; see Error contract). Approval and sender claims / finishes are single bulk statements on the report row in their own transactions (no images) — nothing else to order. Team move (`TeamAdminService.applyDocumentMove`) takes the same direction: the moved team's report rows (id order), then its channel rows (id order), BEFORE the first update; `TechErrorReportService.reorderChannels` locks the team's channel rows in id order first (`TechErrorChannelRepository.lockForTeam`). Before, the move updated channels first and then reports (deadlock with channel delete: reports → channel) and the reorder updated channels in the user's order. Gate `TeamAdminDocumentsTest.moveAllLocksReportsThenChannelsFirst`. Gates `TechErrorReportServiceTest.imageWritersLockTheReportRowFirst` (fails with H2 "Deadlock detected" on the old order), `TechErrorReportPostgresTest.imageDeleteAndReplaceUploadDoNotDeadlock` (NOWAIT probe + concurrent delete / replace rounds). Defence in depth: an image whose topic / image-allowing section no longer exists (`TechErrorReportService.orphanIssueSlot`) is never loaded into the PDF / mail input (`liveImages` reads metas first), never listed on the report screen (`liveImageMeta`), never counted toward the 40 MB, the report list's `image_count` (`imageCounts`: one grouped count + one `iss:` slot query; template / values read only for reports with topic images), the refresh preview's `lost_images` or the refresh's `removed_images` (review 2026-10-18 — the refresh still deletes them); `sweepOrphanIssueImages` (row lock, `IMAGE_DELETE` `topic_removed`, NO content-revision bump — it was never printed) runs before every send (`TechErrorReportSender.send`, a failure never stops the send) and every values save / template refresh deletes them too. A value-too-long on the image insert (slot-id widening patch missing) is 400 `VALUE_TOO_LONG` with a clear text (`GlobalExceptionHandler.CodedBadRequestException`), never a "concurrent change" 409; template refresh keeps them while the topic survives in `carry` and images stay allowed (`keepSlots`; preview `lost_images` counts the rest). The refresh preview also returns `hidden_topics` (`newlyHiddenTopics`: topics that the NEW template leaves out of the PDF / mail — `hide_topic` + impact No, incl. impact inferred from 0 customers — and the old one did not); the confirm says so (`te.applyTemplateHidden`). PDF: under the topic, full width, caption above (none when blank); mail: inside the topic card (`ISSUE_IMAGE_W`), same inline budget / "in the attached PDF" box (the approval request mail captures its "all in the PDF" fallback INSIDE the render loader like the sender — review 2026-10-18: derived from the image-less `rendered.meta()` it said "not uploaded" and dropped topic images; gate `TechErrorApprovalServiceTest.requestMailFallbackKeepsImageBoxes`; when its PDF is NOT attached (over the cap) only the inline images count toward the cap — `(pdfFits ? pdf.length : 0) + inline` — and an image that still doesn't fit gets the `te-image-no-pdf` box with the 20.157.0 text "Görsel bu postaya eklenmedi — görmek için PDF'i Site Monitor'den indirin (tam çözünürlük)." instead of "in the attached PDF" (`buildFull` with `fileName == null`; both sides fixed the same case — the rebase kept main's wording and attachment rule, the separate data-slot is the anomaly branch's); gates `TechErrorApprovalServiceTest.requestPdfNotAttachedKeepsInlineImages`, `TechErrorReportMailTest.imageBoxesWhenPdfNotAttached`. The sender has no such case: it refuses `PDF_TOO_LARGE` before sending, and its preview always passes the file name), slot order = template order with topics in place (`TechErrorMailImages.slotOrder(t, v)`; hidden topics skipped); Content-ID turns `:` into `.` (`te-img-iss.issues.k1.1`).
    - **PDF / mail:** legacy sections unchanged; otherwise title → lead (9 pt) → description → images; the title stays with the first two lead lines (`anomalyTitleNeverOrphaned`) — a topic with neither lead nor description keeps its title with its first image row (`anomalyImageOnlyTopicTitleNeverOrphaned`, the images-section rule); `hide_topic` topics are left out entirely (numbering over printed topics; all hidden → "Bu ay müşteri etkisi olan bir konu yok."); mail spacing via padded cells (the contract rejects 1 px font spacers). The value export / Excel still exclude issues (no aggregate added — one could later report topic count / outage-caused count per month).
    - **History:** `TechErrorChangeDiff.anomalyRows` — date ("12 Eylül 2026"), time range (start / end / next day ONE row, key `i:<sid>:<issue>:time`), outage, outage ID, impact, image captions, labelled with the template's question names; keys merge in auto-save windows like the other topic parts.
    - **Migration `TechErrorAnomalyFieldsMigration`** (marker `2026-10-18-tech-error-anomaly-fields` — never rename after release; waits for `TechErrorCountsInFiguresMigration.KEY` (chain calc → embed → SLA → counts → anomaly); 12 min after boot, hourly, `scheduler_lock` `tech-error-anomaly-fields-migration`, ≤ 24 failed attempts per pod, marker re-read inside the lock, written after ALL channels; `saveTemplate(…, null, "SiteMonitor")`, ≤ 3 retries): adds `IssueOptions.defaults` ONLY to issues sections with no question on and labels + pattern EXACTLY the 2026-10-17 default (TR or EN set; section title ignored); `ratio_of` only when the template has a number field `error-count`. Opened / sent reports keep their copy ("apply latest template" brings the questions; `carry` keeps topic values and infers impact). Team template LIBRARY entries (R21) go through the same rule in the same pass (`migrateLibrary`, review 2026-10-18 — the new-channel dialog preselects the team default, so an unmigrated entry silently created channels without the questions): conditional `TechErrorTeamTemplateRepository.migrateTemplate` on the read `@Version` (+1, "last updated by" SiteMonitor, no audit — one summary log line), ≤ 3 re-reads, unreadable entries skipped (`readStored`, never the system template). A section a team turned ALL off is "configured" (keys written `false`) and is never re-opened. **Old pod / rollout:** a channel created or a library entry saved on an old pod AFTER the marker was written is never migrated (one pass) — "apply latest template" / the template editor ("Varsayılana dön" or the anomaly switches) fixes it. `TechErrorSummaryEmbedMigration` now keeps `options` when it rewrites an issues section (a fresh install's default template went through it). Test oracle `TechErrorTestTemplates.beforeAnomaly(lang)` (= today's default without options; `hostedDefaults` builds on it); the chain tests end with this migration and must reproduce today's default byte-identically.
    - **UI:** `ReportIssues.jsx` (`IssuesBlock` moved out of `ReportSections`; `ImageSlotTile` exported from `ReportImages` with `beforeUpload` / `hideWidth`). Card = title + chips (`te-issue-chips`: date · range, count · share, outage · ID, customers / no impact, "rapora girmeyecek") + groups `te-issue-topic|when|count|outage|impact|desc|images` + lead preview `te-issue-lead`; date via `DateTimeField dateOnly` (shadcn Calendar in Popover, min / max = report month), two `Input type="time"` (side by side on phones, one row ≥ 640 px), shadcn Checkbox "Ertesi gün bitti"; Yes / No as ToggleGroup — Yes reveals the required ID (`req-star`, `aria-required`) / the customer count with an animated reveal and moves focus there when empty; share `<output>` + "Otomatik" badge + formula / reason note; slot 2 appears once slot 1 holds an image; caption input per image; deleting a topic with images asks first. Client validation `issueProblems` / `issueValidation` mirrors the server: invalid values show at once, empty REQUIRED fields (ID, the other end of a range, a date for a range) after a save / PDF / send attempt (`showRequired`); manual save is blocked, autosave sends the linked answers at their stored values (`ISSUE_REVERT_GROUPS`: date / start / end / next day together, outage + ID together). Review 2026-10-18: hidden (not yet shown) required errors still count in the section's invalid badge (`countedErrors`) and are NAMED in the status line (`te.unsavedInvalidList`: "Kesinti ID (<topic>)", ≤ 3 + "+N"; when another field the list does not name — a metrics / table field, a half-typed number — is invalid too, `te.unsavedInvalidListMore` adds "and other invalid fields" instead of "the other fields are saved", review 2026-10-18 round 2); the month name of `dateOutsideMonth` follows the UI language (`issueValidation(t, issues, period, uiLang)`); "Ertesi gün bitti" is disabled until both times exist (`te.iss.nextDayNeedsTimes`); a half-typed time (`validity.badInput`, `TimeInput`) reports through `PendingInputContext` and shows `te.iss.err.timeIncomplete` on blur; topic title is an `h4` (group headings `h5`), topic / title / caption inputs carry `aria-describedby` to their error, the image's accessible name is "<topic> — Görsel n" (`ImageSlotTile contextLabel`); deleting topic image n drops caption n from the draft (`onRemoved`); EN plurals through `.one` keys (`te.iss.chip.count|countShare|customers.one`). `beforeIssueUpload` returns `{ok, reason}` and the slot maps the reason (`ReportImages.UPLOAD_BLOCK_KEYS`): `held` (a restored older backup — `autoHold` — is NEVER saved silently, it would overwrite a newer save), `conflict` (409 / conflict banner), `invalid`, `network`, `server` (`lastSaveFailure` ref in `ReportEditor`). Typing cost: `IssueCard` is `memo` (`sameCardProps`: the card's OWN error-key subset and OWN images compared by value), callbacks are id-based and stable (`updateIssues(sid, fn)` functional `setDraft`), the 40 MB total is read through `imagesRef` at upload time, the period object is memoised (gate `TechErrorIssueCardMemo.test.jsx` counts slot renders). `canonicalValues(v, template)` → `conformIssue` (unasked / hidden answers and `share` never sent — no false "unsaved"). Template editor `AnomalyOptionsEditor` (`te-tpl-anomaly`, shadcn Switch rows with 40 px touch `::after`, labels filled with the template-language defaults, `no_impact_display` choice cards, `ratio_of` NativeSelect of number fields + `te-tpl-anomaly-ratio-warn`; the same warning as a section-header badge `te-tpl-section-ratio-warn` (`ratioOfWarning`, visible while collapsed) and removing a field / section used as `ratio_of` says so in the confirm (`ratioSectionsUsing`, `te.tpl.removeFieldRatioPart`)), `canonIssueOptions` (an off switch equals absence; the editor sends explicit `false` keys, so the server keeps "all off" configured), version diff row `section.anomaly`, layout preview lists the asked questions.
    - **Topic accordion (2026-10-18, user request: "Anormal Artış Gösteren Hatalar altında eklenen konu ve başlıklar da kendi içinde akordiyon gibi açılıp kapanabilsin"):** frontend only, the section-collapse building blocks reused — `TopicOpenContext` (SectionCollapse.jsx) = `useSectionOpenState` + `useSectionOpenValue` (now also `openMany` / `closeMany`), `useUntilFound` (the `hidden="until-found"` + `beforematch` layout effect, extracted from `CollapsibleCard` and shared), `ExpandAllButton` (`slot` / `expandKey` / `collapseKey` props). Open set in its OWN personal-storage key `sm.te.open.ri.<report>` (family `sm.te.open.`, cleared at logout), ids `<section>:<topic>` (`topicOpenId`; neither id can contain `:`) — it cannot collide with section ids, has its own cap `OPEN_TOPICS_MAX_IDS` = 20 sections × 30 topics (600) and is pruned to the report's EXISTING topic ids before every write (review 2026-10-19: `TOPIC_OPEN_OPTS` → `useSectionOpenState(key, ids, {max, prune})`, `pruneOpenState`; an empty / unknown id list prunes nothing; the operation runs after the prune, so a topic added in the same render opens; review 2026-10-19: the prune keeps the SAVED report's topics too — `opts.keep` = `savedTopicOpenIds(base.json, sections)` — so a write while a restored backup draft with fewer topics is shown never drops the saved topics' open state, and after Discard they are open again; "all open" closing still uses the draft ids only) — ids of topics removed by Discard / "apply latest template" no longer fill it and opening a 65th topic never closes another silently (the old shared 64 cap did; `slice(-max)` keeps the newest only as a last guard), and the section "Tümünü aç / kapat" leaves topics as they are. Existing topics start CLOSED; "Başlık ekle" opens the new topic and focuses its Konu input (`IssuesBlock.addTopic`, the id is made outside the updater); removing a topic drops its open entry. `IssueCard` header = `h4` > full-width ghost toggle (`te-issue-toggle`, `CollapsibleTrigger`, chevron first, `pointer-coarse:min-h-10`) holding the title span (`te-issue-title`, the button's `aria-labelledby`) and the summary chips (`te-issue-chips`, spans — phrasing content only — with an sr-only "Konunun özeti:" prefix, the button's `aria-describedby`), so a closed topic still tells its story; copy (`CopyTextButton` of the resolved title) / move / delete stay SIBLINGS of the heading, aligned with its first line (`TOPIC_SIDE`); the header row wraps (`flex-wrap`, title block `grow basis-64`), so on a narrow card (phone, 768 px — four 40 px touch buttons) move / delete drop to a second, right-aligned row instead of squeezing the title (the first e2e run measured a 122 px title at 390). Content (`te-issue-content`, `CollapsibleContent forceMount`) stays mounted when closed (typed invalid text, pending inputs, date pickers, image slots survive; `beforematch` opens). Errors: `reportErrorTopic(key, issues)` maps `issue:<s>:<t>:<f>`, `issues.<s>.<t>.<f>` and the old `issues.<s>.<n>`; a topic whose error set gains a NEW key opens (per key, like sections), a closed topic with errors shows `te.sec.invalid` (`te-issue-invalid`), `revealErrors` (Save / PDF / send refused) opens those topics AND their sections. "Tüm konuları aç / kapat" (`te-issues-all`, `te.iss.expandAll|collapseAll`) next to "Başlık ekle" toggles only that section's topics (`withSectionsClosed`); read-only viewers can collapse too (toggle + toggle-all, no add / move / delete). Cost: `IssuesBlock` passes `open` / `invalid` / `collapsible` as VALUES and a stable `onToggle` (`setOpen` is stable), the topic counts Map changes only with the error set — opening / closing or typing never re-renders another card (gate `TechErrorIssueCardMemo.test.jsx`). Without the provider (no editor) a topic renders open with a plain `h4`. Tests that work inside topics seed them open (`openAllSections()` writes `sm.te.open.ri.<id>` too; `openAllSections({ topics: false })`, e2e `mock(page, { openTopics: false })`). Gates: `TechErrorTopicAccordion.test.jsx`, `techErrorSections.test.js` (topic helpers), `TechErrorIssueCardMemo.test.jsx`, `e2e/channel-reports.spec.js` ("konu akordiyonu", 390/768/1280: closed by default, chips in the header, click + Enter / Space, new topic open + focused, Save opens the invalid closed topic and does not save, toggle-all, no clipping / overflow, ≥ 40 px targets).
    - **Rollback note:** a pre-2026-10-18 pod does not know the issue keys: its lenient read IGNORES them (no error), a template saved there LOSES the questions, a report saved there LOSES the topics' date / time / outage / impact / captions, its "apply latest template" DELETES topic images (`deleteSlotsNotIn` of template slots), it rejects topic-image uploads and prints / mails topics in the old counts layout (topic images not shown). Avoid editing channel templates / reports during the rollout; never roll back past this release without telling the teams (the migration has rewritten default issues sections).
    - **Gates:** `TechErrorTemplateTest.anomalyOptionsStrictAndLenient`, `TechErrorValuesTest.anomalyAnswers|anomalyAnswersFollowTheTemplate`, `TechErrorIssueShareTest` (SHARE_TABLE), `TechErrorIssueTextTest` (LEAD_TABLE), `TechErrorReportServiceTest.anomalyTopicsShareAndImages`, `TechErrorChangeDiffTest.anomalyRows`, `TechErrorReportPdfWriterTest.anomalyLeadAndImages|anomalyHideTopicAndEnglish|anomalyLegacyLayoutUnchanged|anomalyTitleNeverOrphaned`, `TechErrorReportMailTest.anomalyCards|anomalyHideTopicAndLegacy`, `TechErrorAnomalyFieldsMigrationTest` (pure + waits for the counts marker + gives up after 24 + H2 job + `allSwitchesOffStaysOff` + `migratesLibraryEntries`), `TechErrorReportServiceTest.uploadWaitsForTopicRemoval|orphanIssueImagesAreIgnoredAndSwept|refreshPreviewCountsNewlyHiddenTopics|valueTooLongIsRecognised`, `TechErrorApprovalServiceTest.requestMailFallbackKeepsImageBoxes`, `TechErrorReportPdfWriterTest.anomalyImageOnlyTopicTitleNeverOrphaned`, `TechErrorReportControllerTest.anomalyTopicImageAndFieldErrors|topicImageRemovalIsAuditedAndValueTooLongIsClear`, `EmailSamples` (`report-tech-error-tr` carries a full topic + image), `techErrorIssueShare.test.js`, `techErrorIssueModel.test.js`, `TechErrorAnomalyIssues.test.jsx`, `TechErrorAnomalyReview.test.jsx` (upload reasons, caption, UI-language month, badge + status line, next day, half time, headings, plurals, refresh hidden), `TechErrorIssueCardMemo.test.jsx`, `TechErrorTemplateAnomaly.test.jsx`, `TechErrorHistory.test.jsx` (topic-removed note), `e2e/channel-reports.spec.js` ("anomali konusu", "konuyu rapora alma" + template panel, "inceleme 2026-10-18": English UI, Save → required error → fix, keyboard Yes / No, upload refused on 409; 390/768/1280).
  - **Screen capture, paste and the image panel (2026-10-18, user request: "görsel yükle butonuna ek olarak snipping benzeri bir fonksiyon; ekrandan direkt alınabilsin; daha hızlı yöntem varsa onu da yapalım; ayrı bir panelde görsellerin olduğu sayfa açık olacak, site monitör üzerinden diğer sayfaya geçip ekran görüntüsünü alabilmeliyim"):** frontend only (`components/techerror/capture/*`; upload API, validator and permissions unchanged). Every path ends in the slot's OWN `upload(file)` = `prepareImage` + `api.techErrors.uploadImage` (same limits, notes, errors, 40 MB total read at check time, one upload per slot — a paste / crop while the slot is busy says `te.cap.pasteBusy`; a Ctrl/⌘+V paste onto a FILLED slot asks first (`te.cap.replaceTitle|Body`, no undo — review 2026-10-18), whatever the pasted file's type (a typeless PNG would replace it too — review round 2); file pick, drop, "Panodan yapıştır" and the crop confirm do not ask). A 503 `LOCK_CONFLICT` on the upload (panel and main window uploading at the same moment — final review 2026-10-18: a cropped capture had to be taken again) re-sends the SAME prepared file ONCE after the server's `Retry-After` (`lockRetryDelayMs`: `retry_after_seconds` / `retryAfter`, ≤ `LOCK_RETRY_MAX_MS` 3 s, 2 s when absent; no re-prepare, no second `beforeUpload`); if that fails too — or the request was rejected by the network — the server text stays in the slot and the prepared file is KEPT in memory (`te-image-kept`: `te.img.keptHint`, "Yeniden dene" `te-image-retry` re-sends it, "Görseli bırak" `te-image-kept-drop` drops it; a new upload, success or unmount drop it too). Other errors (validation, 4xx) are neither retried nor kept — also when "Yeniden dene" gets one (the kept file is dropped, the server text stays; review 2026-10-19). Before EVERY retry (the automatic one and "Yeniden dene") the slot is RE-READ (`confirmBeforeRetry`: `api.techErrors.report` → the slot's image; the synced list when the read fails): an image that was not there when the upload started (`startId`, kept with the file as `{prepared, startId}`) — the other window just put it there — asks the replace confirmation (`te.cap.replaceTitle` + `te.img.replaceOtherBody`); Vazgeç keeps that image (the sync shows it) and drops the kept file, Değiştir re-sends. Review 2026-10-19: when that image is the user's OWN kept file (a network error after the server had saved it) — `isOwnUpload`: the id the first upload returned when known, otherwise `file_name` (`storedFileName` = the server's `TechErrorReportService.fileName` rule) + `size_bytes` of the prepared file; missing data = not own — the retry counts as done: no confirm, no upload, the kept file is dropped, the list takes the server's image, success toast. A SUCCESSFUL upload always reaches `onImagesChange` + the `images-changed` broadcast, also when the slot unmounted meanwhile (Discard / backup restore / full reload draw a new generation); only the slot's own state and the toast are skipped then; a LATE success never replaces a NEWER image the sync already put in the slot (larger id — review 2026-10-19) (gate `TechErrorImageLockRetry.test.jsx`). Hook into the slot: `useSlotCapture` (`SlotCapture.jsx`) returns `zoneProps` (incl. `ref`) / `zoneRef` / `pasteButton` / `snipButton` / `pasteMenuItem` / `notes` / `emptyHint` / `dialog`; `ReportImages.jsx` only places them. Slot actions (review 2026-10-18, 768 px = two narrow slots side by side): `te-image-actions` is a `@container` (`data-layout` compact | full) — a column of full-width buttons below `@xs`, one wrapping row from `@xs`; a HALF slot keeps "Yükle / Değiştir" (primary) and "Ekrandan al" as buttons and puts "Panodan yapıştır" + "Kaldır" in `KebabMenu variant="labeled"` "Diğer işlemler" (accessible name `te.cap.moreActionsFor` "Diğer işlemler: <slot>", KebabMenu `ariaLabel` prop; no menu when it would be empty); a FULL slot shows all as buttons (`te-image-paste`, `te-image-remove`). KebabMenu items are 40 px on touch (`pointer-coarse:min-h-10`, every kebab). When an upload / delete ends with focus on the body or a disabled / removed control, focus returns to the slot zone (a user who moved elsewhere keeps their focus); the crop dialog returns focus to "Ekrandan al", or to the zone when that button is disabled (upload running). Same gate as upload: read-only / grant VIEW (`can_edit` false) → no paste, no buttons, slot not focusable.
    - **Paste (fastest):** an editable slot is a focusable `role="group"` (`te.cap.zoneLabel`); Ctrl/⌘+V (`paste` event, bubbles from the slot's buttons too) takes the first image item / file (`captureModel.imageFromDataTransfer`), renames it `<slot>-pano-<yyyyMMdd-HHmmss>.<ext>` (Istanbul time) and uploads; a text-only paste is ignored with `te.cap.pasteNoImage`; a NON-image file (a PDF copied in the file manager) goes to the upload path under its own name (`image: false`) so `prepareImage` shows the typed error (`te.img.errPdf` …), nothing is uploaded and nothing is asked. "Panodan yapıştır" (`te-image-paste`) only where `navigator.clipboard.read` exists in a secure context (Chromium, Safari, Firefox 127+; phones too); denied / empty / failed → a muted note naming Ctrl/⌘+V, never a raw error. Hint line in the first images section (desktop or panel): Windows `Win+Shift+S`, macOS `⌘⇧⌃4`, other `PrtSc` (`shortcutFamily`).
    - **Snip session (`captureSession.js`, one per window):** "Ekrandan al" (`te-image-snip`) only with `getDisplayMedia` in a secure context AND (desktop width ≥ 768 OR the panel) — never a broken button. The first press opens the browser picker (video only, ideal 3840 × 2160 / max 8K, ≤ 10 fps, `surfaceSwitching: 'include'`, `selfBrowserSurface: 'exclude'`, `CaptureController.setFocusBehavior('no-focus-change')` when available — getDisplayMedia is the FIRST awaited call so the click's user activation holds); the stream stays live and every later press grabs the CURRENT frame without picking again (`frameReader.readVideoFrame`: first frame ≤ 4 s, then a fresh frame via `requestVideoFrameCallback` ≤ 400 ms — a static window produces no new frames, so the current one is taken; `ImageCapture.grabFrame` only when the video never gets data), at full pixel resolution. Chip `te-cap-chip` "Ekran paylaşımı açık: <source>" + "Durdur" at the top of every images section (panel: sticky in the body); Chrome track labels are machine ids, so `friendlyTrackLabel` falls back to tab / window / screen. Stops (all tracks): Durdur, browser "stop sharing" (`ended` → toast `te.cap.stoppedEnded`), leaving the report / panel (last `retain()` scope released, one tick later — StrictMode / remount keep it), logout / session reset (`utils/sessionResetBus` — `App.resetSessionData` emits, the lazy capture module subscribes; App never imports it), `pagehide`, and `CAPTURE_IDLE_MS` (10 min) without a grab (toast `te.cap.stoppedIdle`) — the idle timer is armed the moment the stream goes `live` (a failed first frame read no longer leaves a stream with no stop; review 2026-10-18). The chip's source name is re-read on every grab and on the track's `configurationchange` (the browser's "share this tab instead" switches the source on the same track). A picker closed while a stop happened stops the arriving stream at once. NotAllowedError / AbortError = quiet note `te.cap.snipDenied`; other errors say what to do (`te.cap.snipFailed`, `snipNoFrame`). Privacy: frames never leave the browser — only the confirmed region is uploaded (hint, crop dialog, help).
    - **Crop dialog (`CaptureCropDialog.jsx`, lazy):** the frame's own canvas is mounted in the stage (no second 4K copy; portal-mounted elements are held in state, not refs). Selection in SOURCE pixels (`captureModel`: `clampRect` / `moveRect` / `resizeRect` / `drawRect` / `keyRect` / `sourceRect` integer edges rounded separately, min 16 px = `SERVER_IMAGE.minSide`); starts as the full frame — dragging on a full selection draws a new one, otherwise inside = move; 8 handles (24 px, 40 px touch, `aria-hidden`); keyboard on the focused stage: arrows 1 px (Shift 10), Alt/Option + arrows resize, Enter uploads; "Tümünü seç", live readout "Seçim: w × h piksel · görüntü W × H" (`te-cap-size`, `aria-hidden`; the screen-reader copy `te-cap-size-live` changes only at drag END, on keys, "Tümünü seç" and a new frame — not on every pointer move), "Yeniden çek" (same stream, selection kept and clamped to the new frame), "Vazgeç", "Bu alanı yükle". A uniform (black / single-colour) frame shows a warning (`isUniformFrame` on a 48 × 27 sample) but stays croppable. Output = PNG at native resolution (`cropFrameToFile`, no smoothing) named `<slot>-ekran-<yyyyMMdd-HHmmss>.png`; focus returns to the slot's "Ekrandan al" (the slot zone while that button is disabled). Stage carries `data-rect` / `data-source` (tests).
    - **Image panel ("Görsel paneli", `ImagesPanelPage.jsx`, lazy):** `?tab=channelreports&te_view=images&te_rep=<id>` (`panelWindow.imagesPanelReportFromSearch`, read once by App) renders a minimal shell — no sidebar / nav / help / tour / dashboard data (loadData, 5-min refresh, network tick, weak-alg and platform reads are skipped), but the same session rules (inactivity strip, passive-account dialog, maintenance layer, 401 path) and page meta `meta.page.imagesPanel`. The page shows only the report's images sections through the SAME `ImagesBlock` (`inPanel`: no panel button, no per-section chip), compact header (report title, period · channel, "Ana pencerede aç" (review 2026-10-18): a same-origin opener gets `postMessage({type: 'sm-te-open-report', rid, reportId, channelId, sentAt}, origin)` and App (`panelWindow.onOpenReportRequest`, installed for every window — panelWindow.js is the import-free entry module) answers AT ONCE, before navigating (`{result: 'opened' | 'blocked'}`; the navigation runs after the ack, so a busy main window never lets the panel's timeout fire after an in-app navigation began), then opens the report IN-APP — `applyTabView` when already on channelreports (the page reads its params only on mount), the normal tab change otherwise, nothing when already on that report. It answers `blocked` and does NOT navigate when the main window has an open window (App modal state `modalCert` / `invForm` / `sharedCert` / `planRow` / `caModal` / `checkRun` / self password, or `panelWindow.hasOpenDialog()` = a `dialog` / `alertdialog` / `aria-modal` element outside Radix poppers — TV mode included) or unsaved work (`utils/unsavedWork.useUnsavedWork` — registered by the report editor, the template editor, the permission matrix and the login-anomaly settings next to their `beforeunload` guard); both windows then toast "close / save it first" (`te.cap.panelMainBusy` in the panel, `te.cap.mainBusyHere` in the main window) — no fallback, a full reload would lose the draft. A request older than `OPEN_REPORT_ANSWER_WINDOW_MS` (timeout − 500 ms) is ignored (the panel will fall back; one navigation only; a request without `sentAt` — older panel bundle — is not age-checked). No ack within `OPEN_REPORT_TIMEOUT_MS` (1.5 s: old pod, logged out, the window is itself a panel, busy) → the old `location.assign` (focus only when already on that report); an opener on another site or closed → new tab AT ONCE (inside the click, so the popup blocker allows it); "Kapat" only when `window.opener` exists), window-level file-drop guard like the editor (`useFileDropGuard` — a file dropped outside a slot used to navigate the popup away), read-only banner `te.cap.panelReadOnly`, 404/403 = the module's "not found / no access" block, no images sections = empty state. Opened from the first images section's HEADER row (`CollapsibleCard headerEnd` → `ImagesPanelHeaderButton`, `te-images-panel-open`; visible while the section is closed — sections start closed; editors, width ≥ 768) with `window.open('', 'sm-te-images-<id>', 'popup,width=520,height=900')` then `location.replace` — an already open panel is focused, never reloaded; blocked → toast with "Görsel panelini yeni sekmede aç" link.
    - **Sync both ways (`imageSync.js`):** `BroadcastChannel('sm-te-images')` (fallback: `sm.te.images.sync` localStorage stamp + `storage`), message `{reportId, kind, from}` only (no image / people data), own messages ignored. `ImagesBlock` posts `images-changed` after every upload / replace / delete (`notifyChange` wraps `onImagesChange`, stable for the memo); the editor posts `report-changed` after "apply latest template" and report delete. Editor (`useImagesSync(reportId, {getImages, apply, localGen})` in `ReportEditor`): on `images-changed` re-reads the report and applies ONLY `images` (draft values, autosave, version untouched; sequence guard), and — when a panel was opened from this window — also on focus / visibility (≤ every 5 s) so a panel closed mid-upload is caught. Review 2026-10-18: an UNCHANGED list (`imageSync.sameImageList`, slot → image id) applies nothing (the editor's own upload callback used to turn `sent` into `changed`, so every refocus after opening a panel showed `te.changedTitle` on an unchanged sent report); a changed list takes the report status from the SERVER's answer (`report.status`). A local upload / delete during the read bumps `localGen` (`onImagesChange`) and the answer is dropped and re-read (≤ `SYNC_RETRY_MAX` 3) — a GET never overwrites a newer local upload. Panel: reloads the whole report on either kind and on focus (≤ every 5 s) — a changed slot list redraws; the same generation guard (`PANEL_SYNC_RETRY_MAX`).
    - **Cross-tab idle (`utils/crossTabActivity.js`, App idle effect):** each tab writes `sm.activity.<tabId>` at most every 15 s on its own activity (user-generated document events only — `wheel`, `pointerdown` (covers `click`), `keydown`, `touchstart`, capture phase so a component stopping propagation still counts, and `mousemove` ONLY when the pointer really moved: `createPointerMoveFilter` keeps the last counted `screenX` / `screenY` and counts a move of ≥ `POINTER_MOVE_MIN_PX` (4) — the synthetic mousemove browsers fire under a stationary cursor when content changes keeps the same coordinates (review round 3: a user reading and only moving the mouse must not be logged out); NOT `scroll`, which browsers also fire on their own when polled content changes height — an unattended visible tab kept the shared session alive (review round 2 2026-10-18; this is also the local idle timer's event list) — + `markActivity()` = window event `sm:activity`, fired by capture / paste / upload); other tabs' `storage` events re-arm the timers WITHOUT writing (no ping-pong) and hide a visible warning; before warning AND before logout each tab reads the newest OTHER stamp (+ the 15 s throttle pad, capped at now) and re-arms from it if newer — so a missed event never logs out early. Own stamp never read → single-tab timing byte-identical; TV mode unchanged (its timer re-arm writes NO stamp — a wall display is not user activity); stamps older than 24 h are pruned. Applies to any two SiteMonitor tabs of the SAME user, not only the panel. Review 2026-10-18: a stamp is JSON `{u, t}` (`u` = `activityUserKey(username)`, a 53-bit hash — the name is never written); only the same user's stamps count, and none when the browser's newest login (`sm.storage.owner`, written by `claimPersonalStorage`, removed at logout) is another user or missing (that tab's session is gone; it will 401). A stamp later than now + `FUTURE_SLACK_MS` (5 s — dual-boot UTC / local clock, VM restore, NTP step) is ignored AND deleted on read and write (it used to read as "now" and disabled idle logout for hours in every tab of the profile); `now < lastWrite` (clock moved back) resets the throttle. The idle effect binds the tab (`bindActivityUser(user)`); `App.resetSessionData` calls `clearOwnActivityStamp()` (own key removed, tab unbound — no write until the next login), so user A's stamps never keep a tab alive for user B. `sm.activity.` is in `personalStorage.PERSONAL_PREFIXES` (the unsalted hash must not stay 24 h after logout): `clearPersonalStorage` (logout) and a new owner's `claimPersonalStorage` delete every stamp.
    - **Anomaly topic images (combined release with the anomaly topics, 2026-10-18):** a topic image slot is the same `SlotTile` (`ImageSlotTile`), so paste / "Panodan yapıştır" / "Ekrandan al" + crop all end in its `upload` — `beforeUpload` (`ReportEditor.beforeIssueUpload`: an unsaved topic is saved first, `autoHold` / conflict / invalid / network reasons via `UPLOAD_BLOCK_KEYS`) runs on every path. The capture name is the topic-aware `fullLabel` ("<topic> — Görsel 1": zone label, replace confirm, "Diğer işlemler" menu); `captureFileName` turns `:` / `_` into `-` (`iss-issues-k1-1-pano-…png`, no `:` in a file name). `IssuesBlock` retains the capture scope (`useCaptureScope` — a report with only topic images stops sharing when left), posts `images-changed` for every topic upload / delete (`notifyChange`, stable for the `IssueCard` memo), draws `CaptureSessionChip` in an image-allowing issues section and — when the report has NO images section — the one `CaptureStopNotice` in the first such section (`ReportEditor.firstIssueImagesId` → `captureNotice`). The image panel lists the image-allowing issues sections in template order (`PanelIssueImages`, `panelIssueSection`): every SAVED topic of the server's values under its title (`h4`, `te-panel-issue`), slot 2 once slot 1 holds an image, no `beforeUpload` (the panel holds no draft; the server accepts only saved topics), `te.cap.panelNoTopics` when none is saved; topics, descriptions and captions stay main-window work. A values save that changes the saved topic ids of an image-allowing section (added topic; removed topic — the server deleted its images in that save) posts `report-changed` (`ReportEditor.imageTopicIds`), so an open panel re-reads at once; other saves post nothing. A topic image deleted in the PANEL drops its caption from the editor's draft (final review 2026-10-18 — it used to stay in the draft and in the saved values): `applySyncedImages` compares the old and the synced image lists, every vanished `iss:` slot (`parseIssueSlot`) goes through `dropIssueCaptions` (same object back when the slot had no caption — no false "unsaved") and its `caption<n>` client error is cleared; a SAVED caption's removal is a change and is saved like any other edit (autosave / Save), a draft-only caption simply returns the draft to its saved state; a read-only view leaves its draft untouched (gate `TechErrorCaptureTopic.test.jsx` "panelde silinen konu görselinin açıklaması"). The editor's `applySyncedImages` bumps `statusGen` and the mail-preview revision like a local change. Gates: `TechErrorCaptureTopic.test.jsx` (paste saves an unsaved topic first, replace confirm names the topic, topic-only report: snip → crop → upload, chip in the issues section, stop notice once, scope stops sharing on leave, topic-list save notifies the panel), `TechErrorImagesPanel.test.jsx` ("anomali konu görselleri"), `captureModel.test.js` (topic file names).
    - **Browser support:** paste: all current browsers; "Panodan yapıştır": Chromium / Safari / Firefox 127+; snip: desktop Chromium / Edge / Firefox / Safari 13+ (no phones, no iPad Safari); panel: desktop widths (≥ 768). `WebConfig` Permissions-Policy restricts only geolocation / camera / microphone / payment — keep display-capture and clipboard-read open.
    - **Gates:** `captureModel.test.js` (incl. non-image file paste), `captureSession.test.js` (picker options, user-activation order, Durdur / ended / idle / scopes / picker-closed stop / session reset, idle timer after a failed first frame, source switch), `captureSync.test.js` (channel + storage fallback, panel URL / open / blocked, main-window message + ack / timeout / cross-origin, `onOpenReportRequest`, `sameImageList`), `crossTabActivity.test.js` (user-bound stamps, future stamps deleted, clock moved back, logout clears, real-move filter), `CaptureCropDialog.test.jsx` (pointer draw / move / handles / touch, keyboard, retake clamp, uniform warning, busy, size announced at drag end), `TechErrorCapture.test.jsx` (paste, text paste, non-image file, replace confirm on a filled slot, busy, clipboard button, half-slot "Diğer işlemler" menu vs full-slot buttons, focus back to the zone, snip → crop → upload, session reuse, Durdur, ended / idle toast, unmount, logout, read-only, phone, unsupported, panel open / blocked), `TechErrorImagesPanel.test.jsx` (only images, sync both ways, template change, read-only, 404, empty, opener message / fallback, window drop guard, editor draft untouched, focus reload, unchanged list on a sent report, local upload during a sync read — editor and panel), `AppCrossTabIdle.app.test.jsx` (two-tab simulation, future stamp, other user, same-coordinate mousemove after it, a real move clears the warning), `personalStorage.test.js` (stamps cleared at logout), `usePageMeta.test.jsx`, `lazyChunks.test.js`, `e2e/channel-reports.spec.js` ("ekrandan görsel alma", 390/768/1280: paste via a dispatched ClipboardEvent; mocked `getDisplayMedia` = a colour-block canvas `captureStream` → drag a region → the uploaded PNG's decoded size = `data-rect` and block-centre colours match (± 14, video colour conversion); second slot reuses the stream; Durdur stops the tracks; panel popup at 520 px uploads and the editor shows the image without reload; half-slot actions inside the slot, "Diğer işlemler" opened / walked / closed by keyboard (focus back on the trigger), menu items ≥ 40 px on touch; "Ana pencerede aç" opens the report in the main window without a page load (a window marker survives); no overflow / clipping, touch targets).
  - **Gates:** `TechErrorTemplateTest`, `TechErrorValuesTest`, `TechErrorImageValidatorTest`, `TechErrorReportPdfWriterTest`, `TechErrorReportServiceTest` (H2, `NOT_SUPPORTED`: content revision, conditional delete, release claim, stale entity save), `TechErrorReportSenderTest`, `TechErrorReportControllerTest`, `TechErrorReportPostgresTest` (concurrent open → 409 with id, CASE/LIKE release), `EmailSamples` (`report-tech-error-tr|en`), `techErrorModel.test.js`, `TechErrorReports.test.jsx`, `TechErrorReportsReview.test.jsx`, `uactModel.test.js` (tab label), `e2e/channel-reports.spec.js` (390/768/1280: fit, no clipping, touch targets, chart grid columns, X → X / Y → Y template).
- **Monthly executive summary (2026-10-10, user request)** — `service/report/executive/*`, `?tab=executive`, `/api/executive-summary[/pdf|/settings|/send-test|/run]`.
  - **Sections are providers:** every `@Component implements ExecutiveSummarySection` is picked up and ordered by `order()` (10 availability, 20 noise, 30 expirations, 40 renewals, 50 `tls-grade` (distribution + month's drops; tier-1 F → critical), 60 `crypto-readiness` (`CryptoInventoryService.summary(null, 10)`; any BROKEN → critical), 70 `data-quality` (`DataQualityService.digest()` + `data_quality_daily` month ends; band → status) — the last three are `snapshot=true`; feature codes print in Turkish in mail/PDF, `ExecutiveSummaryCodeLabelsGateTest` keeps the Java labels equal to `tr.js`). A section returns a generic `SectionResult` (verdicts, KPIs, tables, notes, optional `data`); the mail (`ExecutiveSummaryMail`), the PDF (`ExecutiveSummaryPdfWriter`) and the page render it generically — adding a section needs no other file except TR+EN `exec.<key>.*` keys (`ExecutiveSummaryI18nGateTest` scans the provider sources). Fixed number of aggregate queries per section; inventory / latest certs / team names are read once per summary via `ExecutiveSummaryContext`. Month boundaries are Europe/Istanbul (hourly rollup buckets), never UTC days (daily rollup only as a flagged fallback).
  - **Honest labels:** "SLO" = measured availability (check-weighted, `monitor_check_hourly`) vs the org target, not a formal SLO. Renewal = first sighting of a new fingerprint that EXTENDS validity. **Renewal compliance (2026-10-12, user decision):** `on_time_pct` = renewals made BEFORE expiry ÷ (renewals + certificates that expired IN the month without being renewed, from the fingerprint groups — `RenewalTimelinessSection.lapses`); renewal plans never affect it. "Ahead of target" (`early_pct`, lead left ≥ `renewal-target-days`, 0 = tier lead days) is information only; last minute < 7 days. Sent months show their stored snapshot — "Canlı hesapla" (`live=1`) recomputes with the current rule.
  - **Access:** org summary = global viewer (global admin / AUDIT) AND `executive_summary.view`. Team summary (`?team=<id>`) = also the scoped admin ("müdür") for teams in `viewTeamIds`; USER / TEAM_ADMIN never (even with the matrix grant). No param → org for global viewers, else the first visible team (A→Z); `team=org` without org access → 403. Org settings / test / send = global admin (keys GLOBAL_ONLY); team settings / test / send = global admin or a scoped admin with `canManage(team)`.
  - **Team scope (2026-10-10, user request: "takım bazlı yönetici ayarlaması"):** `ExecutiveSummaryContext` carries `scopeTeamId`; `inventory()` / `latestCerts()` return only rows whose SY OR UG team is the scope team (`allInventory()` / `allLatestCerts()` unfiltered), monitors / alarms filter with `teamInScope` / `domainInScope`; team scope drops the per-team breakdowns and uses team-scope codes (TR+EN `exec.<key>.*`). Org output is byte-identical. `ctx.shared(key, loader)` is a run-wide memo: a delivery run computes org + N teams and reads org-wide raw rows once (`ExecutiveSummaryService.newRunShared`). `ExecutiveSummary.scope` (`org` | `team` + id/name; old snapshots without it read as org) drives mail subject / badge / links (`ex_team`), PDF cover and file name (`fileName(summary)`).
  - **Team recipients and delivery:** `executive_summary_team_settings` (team PK; `enabled` default off, `include_manager` = `teams.manager_id`, `include_team_admins` = AD scoped admins whose manage scope has the team, selected ACTIVE members, extra addresses ≤ 200) — `ExecutiveSummaryTeamService` (fixed queries for any number of teams; passive addresses dropped; note codes `exec.team.note.*`). `/api/executive-summary/teams[/{id}[/send-test|/run]]`, audit `EXECUTIVE_SUMMARY_TEAM_SETTINGS` (run / test / export reuse the existing events with a `team` detail). The same cron sends enabled ACTIVE teams after the org summary, independent of the org switch; each team × month is exactly-once in `executive_summary_team_reports` (UNIQUE team×year×month, same claim/reclaim via the `ExecutiveReportRow` interface and delivery `Slot`s); one team's failure never stops the others; rows older than 24 months are pruned each run; catch-up runs when the org OR any team is on (`existsByEnabledTrue`). Retention: both tables have team-deleted orphan rules. Gates: `ExecutiveSummaryTeamServiceTest`, `ExecutiveSummaryTeamDeliveryTest`, `ExecutiveSummaryServiceTeamScopeTest`, `ExecutiveSummaryControllerTeamTest`, `ExecutiveSummaryTeamMailTest`, the `*TeamScopeTest` section tests.
  - **UI (redesign 2026-10-10):** `components/executive/*` — Tabs "Rapor" / "Alıcılar ve gönderim" (the latter only for configurers; force-mounted, mounted on first visit). Report: scope picker (SearchableSelect) + month, hero (scope chip, status, section-health bar, KPI tiles captioned with their section, key findings), sticky table of contents ≥ 1024 px / scrolling chips below, section cards. Delivery: master list (org + teams, on/off, recipients, last status) → detail (`ExecOrgSettings` / `ExecTeamSettings`); < 1024 px list → detail with a back button; unsaved changes ask before switching. URL `ex_m`, `ex_team`, `ex_live`, `ex_view=delivery`, `ex_sel`; the mail's `ex_cfg=1` opens the delivery view. The tab and menu show for ADMIN (incl. scoped) and AUDIT; the server filters. Gates: `ExecutiveSummaryPage.test.jsx`, `e2e/executive-summary.spec.js` (390/768/1280, both views).
  - **Delivery:** opt-in (default OFF); dynamic cron (`0 0 9 1 * *`) + hourly catch-up (72 h) under `scheduler_lock` "executive-summary"; exactly once per month via `executive_summary_reports` UNIQUE(year, month) + conditional `reclaim` (FAILED/NO_RECIPIENT retried ≤ 3, a durable "sending started" marker is written BEFORE the first BCC chunk (multi-pod R2a-D1, MP-42, review REP-1): `markSendStarted` = a conditional UPDATE on this claim's SENDING row (`send_started_claim` = the claim's `claimed_at`, `send_trace_name` = the `notification_logs.recipient_name` the chunks use, planned recipient / chunk count; columns `executive_summary_reports|executive_summary_team_reports.send_started_claim|send_trace_name`, entity `insertable/updatable=false`, `ReportIntegritySchemaPatch`); if it cannot be written (3 tries) or the claim moved (0 rows) NO chunk is sent (FAILED + trace / NOT_CLAIMED); `reclaim` resets it. A SENDING row claimed ≥ 1 h ago (the sending pod died) is settled by the hourly catch-up from the marker and the chunk TRACES (`BCC×` rows, trigger `EXECUTIVE_SUMMARY`, `recipient_name` = the STORED trace name — a team renamed in between is still found —, `sent_at >= claimed_at`): no marker for this claim AND no trace → nothing went out: the row is written FAILED by a conditional UPDATE and the same catch-up resends (counts an attempt; at `MAX_ATTEMPTS` it stays FAILED + trace); marker AND a trace for EVERY planned chunk → the result from the traces (all failed → FAILED, retryable — really nothing went out; all sent → SENT) + `SKIPPED: Gönderen pod sonucu yazamadan durdu …`; anything else (marker with missing traces — a DB blip swallowed the traces, the pod died mid-chunk — or traces without a marker — an old pod) → the result is UNKNOWN: PARTIAL + `FAILED: Sonuç bilinmiyor … yeniden gönderilmedi`, NEVER resent (at most once; a global admin decides with "Şimdi gönder", which still takes over a stale SENDING row); the trace query unreadable → the old rule (manual only). Finishing a row is a conditional UPDATE (`finishIfClaimed`: `status = 'SENDING' AND claimed_at = <this claim>`, all result fields) retried 3 times — never a whole-entity save; 0 rows = the claim moved to another sender. Scheduled / catch-up claims skip a row claimed < 30 min ago (`MIN_RECLAIM_SPACING`). The planned fire runs once per cluster (`claimCronRound("executive-summary")`) and only when the LIVE cron fires at the trigger's PLANNED instant (`service/report/LiveCron.PlannedFire` records what the trigger computed; `firesAt` compares the live cron with that instant — review REP-3: comparing the wall clock with a 61 s window took a tick delayed by the busy 8-thread scheduler for a changed schedule, and the monthly inventory report, which has no catch-up, was lost; a late tick now sends with a WARN; a cron changed after the trigger was planned still doesn't send at the old time; the wall-clock rule is only the fallback when no planned instant is known); the catch-up once per hour per cluster (`claimRound("executive-summary-catchup", 55 min)` + `completeRound`). The monthly inventory report (`CertInventoryReportScheduling`) uses the same planned-fire check + `claimCronRound("cert-inventory-report")`. Rolling deploy: old pods fire without the cluster round / planned-fire check (the month row's UNIQUE claim still prevents a second executive mail) and finish with a whole-entity save (only on their own claim); an old pod writes no marker, so a stale old-pod row WITH traces is "unknown" (PARTIAL, not resent) and one WITHOUT traces is resent (the old pod may have died between an SMTP accept and its trace — accepted for the rollout window; a new pod's own sends are at most once); the marker is matched to the claim's `claimed_at`, so an old pod's `reclaim` that does not reset it never applies it to a new claim. Gates: `ExecutiveSummaryDeliveryServiceTest.staleSendingWithoutTrace_catchUpResendsOnce|staleSendingWithTraces_partialNoResend|staleSendingAtMaxAttempts_failedNoResend|finishWriteRetried|reclaimSpacing|markerWrittenBeforeFirstChunk|markerNotWritten_nothingSent|staleSendingStartedWithoutTraces_unknownNeverResent|staleSendingAllTracedFailed_incompleteNeverResent`, `ExecutiveSummaryTeamDeliveryTest.renamedTeam_tracesFoundByStoredName`, `ExecutiveSummarySendMarkerQueriesTest` (H2 JPQL), `ExecutiveSummarySchedulingTest` (incl. `lateTrigger_stillRuns|runnableUsesPlannedFire`), `CertInventoryReportSchedulingTest.latePodSameFire_noSecondMail` (two real `SchedulerService` pods on one H2 — the cron round) `|lateTrigger_stillSends|runnableUsesPlannedFireFromTrigger|oldFireAfterCronChange_notDue`; an unexpected error after the claim finishes the row as FAILED/PARTIAL instead of leaving SENDING; an org-path error never stops the team loop; manual send of the CURRENT, unfinished month is rejected with 400 `month` and the button is disabled — 2026-10-12); BCC chunks of 100 with the PDF; passive users' addresses dropped; every chunk / skip in `notification_logs` (`EXECUTIVE_SUMMARY`, test `EXECUTIVE_SUMMARY_TEST`); system-maintenance mute is NOT applied (same as the other reports). Sent months store `summary_json` and the page shows that snapshot (`source=snapshot`) unless `live=1`.
  - **Correctness rules (2026-10-12):** a month is sendable only after 06:00 Istanbul on the 1st of the NEXT month (the nightly roll-up runs at 03:00): manual send → 400 `month`, scheduled run / catch-up → `DATA_PENDING` (`ExecutiveSummaryService.dataReady`, UI `runHintKey`). Base loads (latest certs, inventory, roll-ups, overview rows, section queries) THROW instead of returning empty, so a read failure is a section ERROR, never NO_DATA or a clean verdict; scheduled / catch-up delivery with an ERROR section finishes FAILED and is retried (sent anyway on the last of `MAX_ATTEMPTS`; manual send always sends). Org recipients with an unreadable global-admin list, or team recipients whose teams / settings / members / managers can't be read, send NOTHING (no claim, catch-up retries; manual team send → `RECIPIENTS_UNAVAILABLE`). Paused inventory domains stay mapped in availability. A month already SENT / PARTIAL / SENDING is not claimable, so a scheduled re-run reads no recipients and writes no "unreadable" trace; that trace is written once per month per pod (review round 5); the scheduled path returns ALREADY_SENT / IN_PROGRESS from the row itself without trying a claim (review round 6). The TLS-grade section's drop log is a base load (throws → section ERROR); only its coverage note is optional. PDF table text wraps (`PdfCanvas.wrapCell`, ≤ 6 lines, long tokens split). Settings forms send only changed fields (`utils/changedFields.js`). A deleted / unknown `ex_team` falls back to the default scope; the toast shows only after it loaded.
- **Admin runtime settings** — `AppSettingsService` (curated key/value, catalog in `AppSettingsCatalog`, live-reloads CORS), `SmtpSettingsService`/`SmtpMailService`, `GeneralSettingsController`, `DatabaseInfoService`, and `SecretCipher`/`SecretToolsService` (AES-GCM decrypt of stored SMTP/LDAP passwords, gated by `SITE_MONITOR_SECRET_KEY`). These settings + secret-tools endpoints are bootstrap-admin only.
- **Secret key rotation (2026-10-11, user request).** `site.monitor.secret-key-previous` (`SITE_MONITOR_SECRET_KEY_PREVIOUS`, Helm `secret.secretKeyPrevious`) is DECRYPT-ONLY: `SecretCipher` encrypts with the current key and decrypts current → previous (GCM tag decides; no key id). The stored format is unchanged (`enc:v1:`) so a rollback still reads values — never introduce a new prefix. `SecretRotationService.LOCATIONS` is the single list of persisted encrypted values (LDAP, SMTP, HTTP/PageSpeed basic auth + headers, keyword headers, scripted env + script versions, push headers app_setting); `SecretLocationsGateTest` fails when a model gains a `*_enc` column or a new file calls `encrypt(` without a location. Status (`GET /api/admin/secret-tools/rotation`) counts current / previous / unreadable per location (keyset pages of 500, ≤ 100k rows); re-encrypt (`POST …/rotation/reencrypt`, `{"confirm":true}`, `scheduler_lock` `secret-reencrypt` via `SchedulerService.callWithSchedulerLock`, 409 codes `SECRET_KEY_NOT_CONFIGURED` / `PREVIOUS_KEY_NOT_CONFIGURED` / `REENCRYPT_RUNNING`) rewrites ONLY previous-key values with a conditional `UPDATE … WHERE id=? AND col=<read value>` (singletons + app_settings also bump `updated_at` so other pods' caches refresh); audit `SECRET_REENCRYPT` carries counts only. OTP: `OtpCodes.matches` also accepts the previous key's sub-key (both HMACs always computed); no sub-key is derived from the public dev key. UI: `admin/secret/SecretRotationPanel` inside Secret tools (phases `dev_key|pending|done|incomplete|done_unreadable|unreadable|count_incomplete|steady`, KPI cards filter the location list, unreadable records listed by name). Procedure: docs/RUNBOOK.md → Anahtar döndürme. Gates: `SecretCipherRotationTest`, `SecretRotationServiceTest` (H2, real SQL), `SecretRotationPostgresTest`, `SecretToolsControllerTest`, `SecretRotationPanel.test.jsx`, `secretRotationModel.test.js`, `e2e/secret-rotation.spec.js`.
  **2026-10-12:** re-encryption resumes each location where the previous run stopped (row cap or the 8-minute budget → `partial`, cursor per pod; the row cap counts WRITE attempts, not rows read — a pod without the cursor skips current-key rows cheaply and still progresses, review round 4); `can_reencrypt` stays on while a count is truncated; with no previous key a location error / truncated count shows `count_incomplete`, never `steady`. Gates: `SecretRotationServiceTest.reencryptResumesAcrossRuns`, `secretRotationModel.test.js`.
- **Diagnostics** — `OpensslDiagnosticsService` / `NetworkDiagnosticsService` / `HstsDiagnosticsService` / `ConnectionDiagnosticsService`, persisted by `DiagnosticHistoryService` (`diagnostic_runs`); surfaced via the DiagnosticsModal under `/api/admin/diagnostics/*`.
- **HTTP end-to-end diagnosis (2026-10-02)** — `POST /api/monitoring/http/{id}/diagnose` (+ `/diagnose/history[/{runId}]`), `HttpDiagnosticsController` + `service/http/diagnose/*` (`RawHttpProbe` raw-socket HTTP/1.1 trace: DNS, TCP/proxy, CONNECT, TLS chain/trust, exact request/response headers, redirect hops, 32 KB text body preview, curl -v transcript; `HttpDiagFindings` code catalogue; `HttpDiagMasker`). Runs the monitor's own route and, when a proxy is configured, the other route in parallel (`PATH_DIFFERS` = the 2026-10-02 node/egress incident in one click), plus `client_check` = the real `HttpCheckerService.checkForDiagnostics` (no CA auto-pin, no trust-failure recording, existing `check()` untouched). Side-effect free: no `http_checks` rows, no alarm evaluation. Gate = `diagnostics.run/execute` + `SessionScope.canOperateTeam` (list rows carry `can_diagnose`); 6/min per user and per monitor, max 4 in flight. History in `diagnostic_runs` (`run_type=HTTP_DIAG`, domain `http-monitor:<id>`, so the admin cert history never lists them), body previews never stored. Secrets (auth/cookie/encrypted custom headers, Set-Cookie values, proxy/basic-auth passwords) are masked everywhere incl. the transcript. UI: `components/http/diagnose/*` (lazy dialog from the HTTP detail header "Uçtan uca tanıla" and the failure-diagnosis footer; never auto-runs; `hdx=<runId>` opens a stored run). A new finding code needs `httpdx.finding.<CODE>.title|body` in tr.js + en.js (`HttpDiagFindingsI18nGateTest`); `params.reason` variants use `…<part>.<reason>` keys.
- **Certificate window → "Değişiklikler" tab (2026-10-05, user request: "who added / changed a certificate monitor, when").**
  - **Source:** no new data source. Inventory add / edit / delete / restore are already written to the monitor change log (`MonitorHistoryService.INVENTORY`) and the audit log.
  - **UI:** `certmodal/CertChangesTab.jsx` resolves the inventory record by domain (`/admin/inventory/by-domain`) and shows an "Ekleyen / Son güncelleyen" card (`created_*` / `updated_*` columns), plus the shared `ChangeHistoryTab` (kind `inventory`, field-level before → after). Team names come from `/api/teams/directory`.
  - **Visibility:** same rule as the Inventory drawer — the tab is hidden for another team's (read-only) record and in preview mode.
  - **Legacy:** records older than the change log show only the added / last-updated card.
  - **Gates:** `CertChangesTab.test.jsx`, `e2e/cert-detail-stability.spec.js` (9 tabs, the changes tab fits at 390 / 768 / 1440).
- **Uploaded (manual) certificates (2026-10-06, user request: certificates with no network endpoint — JKS keystores/truststores on OCP, vendor PEMs — tracked from an uploaded file).** **Analysis matches are scoped (2026-10-22, UIM-24 / K6):** `POST /api/manual-certs/analyze` `entries[].matches.*` are limited to records the caller can read (`ManualCertificateController.canRead`) via `ManualCertificateAnalyzer.Analysis.applyScope(MatchScope)` (single filter); `already_tracked` and every `same_subject` item carry `can_write` (the renew gate); hidden matches also drop their warnings; the create refusal stays org-wide but a 409 `ALREADY_TRACKED` for a record the caller can't read carries no `domain` / `inventory_id`.
  - **Model:** a normal `certificate_inventory` row with `cert_source='MANUAL'` (NULL = network row, behaviour byte-identical; `isManual()`); the key is a tracking key (`ManualCertificateKeys`, `^(\*\.)?[a-z0-9]([a-z0-9._-]{0,251}[a-z0-9])?$`, class-level `@InventoryDomainKey` keeps the old regex for network rows). Versions in `manual_certificate_versions` (`is_current`, one current per row — partial unique index on PostgreSQL; PUBLIC chain PEM only). Private keys and passwords are NEVER stored, logged, audited or returned.
  - **Private keys and keystore passwords NEVER leave the browser (2026-10-08, user decision: "keystore yüklemesinde kesinlikle private key için bir yükleme yapmayalım").** The wizard extracts the public certificates client-side (`components/manualcert/extract/*`, Web Worker, lazy chunk; own BER/PKCS#12 walker that decrypts ONLY cert bags — PBES2 AES / legacy 3DES + RC2 via `node-forge` primitives, never forge's pkcs12/RSA modules; own JKS/JCEKS reader that skips protected key bytes; `fflate` for ZIP; BKS/UBER unsupported with a keytool guide) and every upload call sends only the `extracted` JSON part (`{format, file_name, size_bytes, entries[{alias,key_entry,certs[b64 DER]}], csr_pem[], private_keys_removed}`), validated strictly by `ExtractedUpload` (unknown fields such as `password` rejected, whitelist formats, ≤ 200 certs, DER X.509 only, no "PRIVATE KEY"). The legacy raw `file`/`text` path accepts certificate-only PEM/DER/PKCS#7/ZIP and rejects PKCS#12, JKS/JCEKS/BKS and anything with a private key with 400 `PRIVATE_KEY_NOT_ACCEPTED`; there is no server-side keystore/password code any more. Warning `PRIVATE_KEY_KEPT_LOCAL {count}`. Never add a code path that sends a key or a keystore password to the server.
  - **One tracked certificate per chain (2026-10-07, user decision):** `ManualCertificateAnalyzer` folds every certificate that appears in another one's built chain into that head; `entries` = chain heads only (leaf+int+root ⇒ 1, int+root ⇒ 1, two unrelated roots ⇒ 2), each with `preview` (offline SSL-tab map) and `certificate_count`.
